### 8.9 T1 of the Tauride remake: the Palace and the Paradny quarter

Phase T1 of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (its log, section 12, has the commit list and the numbers; this section is the part that belongs to the area docs). Branch `tb-t1`, no engine file touched.

* **The module** `landmarksTauride` = `src/world/landmarksTauride.js` + `landmarksTauride/*.js` (8.1). `build(ctx)` makes three parts: `tauride-palace` (the six-column portico, the drum and the dome, the wings: `PALACE_PARTS`), `paradny-quarter` (the 11 buildings), `paradny-courts` (the 3.3 m podium floor, its walls with openings at the mapped stairs, eight stairs with their handrails, the landings, the two black-granite fountain basins with 13 jets each, beds, benches, lanterns).
  It publishes `ctx.services.landmarksTauride = { paradny: { deckY, podium, fountains, jets }, deckY, triangles }`: `deckY(x, z)` is the Paradny deck (3.3 m on the podium, the ramps of the eight stairs, `null` elsewhere), asked FIRST by cars (`drive/handling.js` through `HOOKS.deckServices`) and by pedestrians (`people/nav.js surfaceY`); **no `entrances`** (the palace and the quarter have no door points; the source's `entrances` were the garden gates, so the module is not in `PRUNE_SERVICES`).
  Numbers (`npm run check:north`, 8.5): 621,713 triangles in 52 meshes, 234 instances (the jets), 1,270 colliders, build 0.8-1.0 s; BUDGET 700 k.
* **What the module switches on** (the M6 hooks of section 9 are ACTIVE from T1 on): `HEIGHT_PATCHES = [paradny]` (box [741, 1058.51, -1205, -873]), the tree / tuft / prop keep-outs, the pedestrian-only rule (56 service ways), the parking reject, the deck service. Measured on the page's real list, env snapshot (`tools/north-env-snapshot.mjs`; new baseline `D:/git2/n4spb-work/tauride/after/env-snapshot-north.json`):

  | | trees | tuft cells | props | parked slots / bay lines | pedestrian-only ways | lanes | lamps | podium ring: trees / props / tuft probes |
  |---|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
  | before T1 (`feat/areas` 4eb2a0e) | 10,884 | 2,790 | 1,854 | 2,448 / 1,161 | 13 | 10,499 | 1,042 | 8 / 6 / 267 |
  | the M6 what-if (hooks only, module not built, 9.5) | 10,877 | 2,747 | 1,860 | 2,448 / 1,161 | 69 | 10,289 | 1,042 | 0 / 1 / 0 |
  | T1, claims only (module listed, every hook deleted) | 10,914 (the M5 keep-outs are deleted too) | 2,810 | 1,872 | 2,448 / 1,161 | 13 | 10,499 | 1,042 | 3 / 1 / 267 |
  | **T1, real** | **10,893** | **2,746** | **1,872** | **2,447 / 1,159** | **69** | **10,289** | **1,042** | **0 / 1 / 0** |

  Everything inside the podium and on the streets equals the M6 what-if (the podium holds no tree and no tuft, the one prop left is a mapped bench, 56 ways lose their lanes, 210 lanes fewer, one slot and two bay lines fewer): `north-env-snapshot --diff <claims-only> <real> --allow all --expect-empty paradny,campus,suvorov` = RESULT OK (1,175 changes, all inside the allowed regions or their cascade).
  **What the what-if could not show is the CLAIM effect**: a module in the build list claims its buildings (`lib/claims.js`), so `city` stops drawing the palace and the 11 buildings (generic city 803,387 -> 787,005 triangles) and `environment` stops planting their courtyards: 374 -> 366 courtyard trees, 358 -> 356 yard trees, and the tree streams of the woods / props re-roll (470 tree differences outside every keep-out region, clustered round the palace at x 1100-1500, z -1800 .. -1400; props 1,854 -> 1,872 although the hook changes no prop any more: the claims already take 5 of the 6 props of the snapshot out of the podium: 4 of 5 in the lists of `north-keepout-test`, which is why its prop check proves the hook with a fifth variant, `fake` = `off0` + `propReject` alone, no claims: 5 props in the podium -> 1, and a dead `propReject(cx, cz)` call site in `props/world.js` fails the check). The re-rolls are the source's behaviour (one random stream per GREEN polygon / courtyard, one stream for all props), not a hook leaking: `--allow` only passes them as "(cascade)" when the module is built with its hooks, and the strict diff against the M6 baseline is therefore FAIL by design from T1 on (re-take the baseline: the one above).
* **The strip is unchanged** (all oracles of 9.6 re-run at the T1 head): `layout-golden --diff` identical, `npm run check` identical in 25 modules, `streets-signature` / `city-signature` / `environment-regress` +0, `north-env-snapshot` strip identical (6,206 trees, 2,866 tuft cells, 2,738 props, 3,184 slots, 5 pedestrian-only ways, 12,860 lanes, 1,407 lamps); `owns-table.js` gains one row that only counts where a page lists the module.
* **The two copies of the deck** (the module's `paradny-surface.js` and `src/areas/north/hooks/paradny.js`) are held equal by the `paradny twin` group of `tools/north-hooks-test.mjs` (CI, needs no three.js): the exports, the golden lattice `tools/golden/paradny-deck.json` (1286 x 1344 points, hash 4bda374859cd45b9) and 498,703 direct probes (a 0.5 m lattice over the box + 3 m, 60,000 random points, NaN and the edges) answer bit for bit; mutants (landing step +.015 -> +.016, stair width 1.8 -> 1.9, the 0.9 m cut -> 1.0) each fail 2-3 checks.
* **The North layout golden** was regenerated (`tools/golden/layout-north.json`): the new sub-groups, the five moved VIEWS poses, and the probe sets (+2 points: the anchors of the two new sub-groups; heightAt at (944, -1038) is the 3.3 m deck). `heightAt` against the old line (waterway bed / 0) on a 2 m lattice over the whole North + 150 m (1,817,508 points): 18,570 points differ, all inside the patch box and all equal to `paradnyDeckY`, none outside.
* **The tripwires of M6 that said "no landmarksTauride in MODULES" were flipped on purpose** (never deleted): `north-hooks-test` (the parkingReject / HEIGHT_PATCHES / heightAt checks no longer accept both states; a new `dormant` child proves the old behaviour with the list minus the module; the what-if baseline is the old line), `north-keepout-test` (variants `off0` / `dormant` = the list without the module, `off` / `real` = the page's list, so that the claims are never mistaken for a hook, and `fake` = `off0` + `propReject` alone, the only way to see that hook while the claims already clear the podium), `north-people-hooks-test` (the generic-North check strips the module from a copy of `area.js`), `north-height-test` (the patch must be listed and no pose of `VIEWS` may stand on the deck).
* **Views**: `Digit4` (915, -1010) stood ON the podium: with the patch the ground is 3.3 m and `tools/views-check.mjs` accepts -0.5 .. 1.5 only. The podium is the better vantage (the ring buildings are visible only from inside: from the street the quarter shows 8-34 % of its facade samples), so the lead may prefer to keep a podium pose and let `views-check` accept the height of an active patch; the pose was moved because `views-check.mjs` was not part of T1.
  Candidates scored with line-of-sight checks (scratch tools, buildings by height, the podium wall, tree crowns, poles / signals / lamps / plates in the view cone): Digit4 (900, -852) yaw 0: the two south blocks r3304715 / r3304716 fully visible (40 of 40 facade samples) at 36-80 m, nothing in the way along the central axis (the first building ahead is 216 m away); alternatives (1052, -892) yaw 37 (the SE corner, 34 %), (906, -852) yaw 19, (1112, -1100) yaw 90 across the east avenue (the 13 m offices hide the ring behind). Digit0 (1224, -1266) yaw -173: the street cross-section there is 20 m wide (z -1264.3 .. -1244.3, both pavements: views-check refuses the pavement), so a pose 15-25 m back is impossible; this is the nearest valid one on the garden side (28 m, 74 % of the campus frontage samples visible against 25 % at the old pose, nothing in the cone against four wall colliders 7-8 m away), axial on the house church; alternatives (1220, -1266) yaw -147 and (1244, -1266) yaw 142 (80 %, oblique), (1258, -1266) yaw 158 (74 %, 36 m). It lies 1.8 m south of the south gate line of the garden (1226, -1267.8): the later phases put the fence there, so re-check it with the fence (the HUD shows "Tauride Garden" there, not "Kirochnaya"). Digit3 (-167, -1157) yaw -107: Dom Muruzi fully visible at 32 m, no pole or signal within 14 m in the cone; alternatives (-165, -1183) yaw -124, (-181, -1169) yaw -129 (both with 44-47 m of road in front), the old pose scores a pole at 1.3 m (34 degrees right) and a signal at 9 m.
* **Known behaviour of the source, not changed (the six files are verbatim)**: the podium is a wall from the street (0 of 197 walkers and 0 of 104 cars got onto it in 8 s / 7 s runs toward the edge, except through the stairs) but not from above: the parapet colliders are boxes 0 .. 3.3 m and `core/physics.js` skips a box whose top is within `STEP_HEIGHT` of the feet, so a walker or a car on the deck passes over the edge (208 of 240 walker runs and 104 of 119 car runs ended at street level, a 3.3 m drop; the source module in the same harness: 214 of 240 walkers). 13 of those cars were wedged against the podium wall below (they could not reverse within 3 s). A collider top of `Y + 1.0` in `paradny-courts.js` (a railing: invisible, no change of the meshes) would stop both; it is a deviation from the verbatim copy and left to the lead. AI traffic never reaches the podium: the 12 lanes that have a point in the polygon (six service ways, the three straddling ways of the M6 lens among them) are not in the traffic sim at all (service lanes carry no AI cars), and 5 cameras x 960 s of the regional sim (`createSim`, as the game runs it) put 0 vehicles inside the polygon. Pedestrians: 6 minutes with the player on the podium, nobody inside the polygon (the nav lattice does not know the podium; T7). `node tools/tauride/paradny-walk.mjs`: 8 stairs, 0 failing.

### 8.10 T2 of the Tauride remake: the land relief, the terrain mesh, the car keep-out, the claims of the garden

Phase T2 of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (its log, section 12, has the commits, the numbers and the commands; section 7.1 the relief table). Branch `tb-t2`. LAND relief only: the pond beds, the bank slopes and the water come in T4.

* **The relief** is authored data (`src/world/landmarksTauride/data/relief.js`: 9 elliptical features, each with a provenance `osm` | `design` and its evidence; ALL heights are design values) evaluated by the PURE function `gardenHeight(x, z)` (`landmarksTauride/height.js`: its own box first, then a cell mask of 4 m cells that names the one or two hills which can reach the point, no allocation, deterministic, `null` where no hill lifts the ground; equal bit for bit to the brute-force evaluation of every feature, `tauride-height-test`). `hooks/height.js` lists it as the second patch (`garden`, after `paradny`) with the box `RELIEF_HULL` (the hull of the nine hills, 1031.5 .. 1473.5 x -1728.5 .. -1303.5 = 62 % of the garden box: T2 fix round 1, so that `layout.heightAt` makes no call for the rest and the start-up cost stays inside the +30 % gate of the plan, docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md section 12); `layout.heightAt` therefore answers the hills inside the garden box and the old line everywhere else (over the whole North + 150 m on a 2 m lattice: 21,827 points differ from the old line, all inside the Paradny box or the garden box and all equal to their patch).
* **The terrain mesh** (`landmarksTauride/terrain-mesh.js`): an adaptive, crack-free, 2:1-balanced quadtree over `gardenHeight` (<= 5 cm, leaves of 1-16 m, no triangle where the ground is within 2 cm of flat, and a leaf that lies across the foot of a hill splits down to 2 m, so the mesh follows the 2 cm contour: the first version kept every 16 m leaf with one lifted sample, an apron of flat triangles (25 % of the footprint) that showed as a straight-edged "pad" of placeholder colour round every hill in the aerial views), one mesh per 64 m chunk merged down to at most 16 draws, `noBatch` + `registerLOD(Infinity)` like the chunks of `environment/ground.js`, the GROUND'S OWN shader (fix round 2: `terrainMaterial` finds the material of the mesh `env-ground` in the group `env` of `ctx.staticRoot` and gives the relief a second `ctx.mat.toon` material that carries the ground's `onBeforeCompile` and `customProgramCacheKey`, so it is the same compiled program with the same uniform objects and the colour, a function of the world (x, z) alone, is the lawn and the way paint of the flat ground at the same place: no seam along the contour; it has NO polygonOffset, the ground has +2, so the relief keeps winning over it; without an environment ground, a headless test of the builder alone, it is the old vertex-coloured placeholder), NO colliders. It adds 5,479 triangles and 16 meshes to the module (footprint 13,736 m2 for 12,208 m2 lifted by 2 cm or more).
* **The claims**: the module's third sub-group `tauride-garden` (`groups.js`, literal ids): the park r1582734, the 23 woods of `GARDEN_GREEN`, the 120 garden ways (bridge ways included), (T3 fix round 1) the four footway AREAS inside the fence (`w415779476` the central round, `w1458068090`, `w1458068100`, `w1458068120`: `streets` drew each as a kerb wall round a back-face-culled paver cap), the 132 benches and 167 bins. `props/osm.js` skips a claimed bench / waste basket (`L.isClaimed(f.id)`, one line; `L.isClaimed` is the STATIC claim table of the area's GROUPS, it does not look at the build list, so the skip acts in every North build, also one without the module); 0 of the strip's 561 claimed ids is a FURNITURE node (`tools/tauride/strip-furniture-claims.mjs`), so the strip is unchanged. The skip is guarded by `north-keepout-test` (fix round 1: the variants place 0 of the 299 records on their nodes or inside the outline, the positive control `free` = the records taken out of the claim places 288). Until T3 / T5 / T6 the generic North draws no tree, grass tuft, hedge, path ribbon or OSM bench in the garden: it is bare terrain (measured, `env-snapshot`: trees 10,893 -> 7,121, tuft probes of the garden 3,160 -> 0, props 1,872 -> 1,560; nothing generic stands on a hill: 0 trees, 0 props, 0 tuft probes).
* **Cars** (`HOOKS.carKeepOut`, 9.1): `drive/handling.js solidBlocked` refuses the garden polygon; with the real `createHandling().blocked`, 0 m of the 2,158 m outline can be driven across (2,018 m without the hook), the service lane is not an exception. **That is the static predicate; fix round 2 found that the STEP could still carry a car over the line (QA: the bus stop on the south edge, x 1388 .. 1405, a car heading NW at 30 .. 36 degrees)** and closed two holes in `sub()`: (1) `collide(car)` (the contact push of a solid, here the bus shelter between the lane and the fence) moves the car's origin AFTER the keep-out test and nothing re-checked it; (2) the 'already embedded' rule (a blocked pose may move while the count of blocked footprint points does not grow) saturates at nine points, and a car with all nine inside the polygon was free to roam, 100+ m into the garden. Now `keepPoints(x, z, sy, cy)` counts the footprint points (centre + eight) in the keep-out (0 for the strip: no hook), the contact push is UNDONE when it makes the count grow (the impulse stays), and the embedded rule gets a second condition: the keep-out count may not grow, whatever the other blocked points do (the keep-out is a closed ground: no embedded escape across its line; a car that STARTS inside may still drive out, and cannot drive back in). Strip: `KEEP_OUT` is undefined, the new code is a falsy test. Proof, dynamic: `tools/tauride/car-keepout-sim.mjs` drives real cars (coupe, roadster, truck) at the REAL North (12 modules built headless, 37 k colliders) from 60 points of the outline at seven headings (plain / nitro) = 2,192 runs of 8 s (`--full`; the default mode is 36 points x 5 headings = 900 runs, about 100 s), and the 110-run bus-stop grid: 0 breaches (the copy without the undo, M1 below: 423 of 2,192 and 45 of 110, up to 9 of 9 footprint points inside), plus three synthetic worlds (a box between the lane and the ground, a footprint embedded in a building that touches the ground, a car that starts inside); two copies of `handling.js` with one guard cut out (M1 the undo, M2 the embedded term) must breach the same scenarios.
* **Pedestrians** (`people/nav.js surfaceY`, 4 lines behind `HEIGHT_PATCHES.length`): where the terrain is lifted a walker stands ON it (`heightAt + 0.02`), uphill and downhill; before, nodes above feet + 2.45 m sank to street level (-4.4 m on the north hill). The strip has no patch, so the branch never runs there.
* **Proof that the strip is untouched**: `layout-golden --diff` (strip) identical, the strip's `npm run check` identical in all 25 modules, the strip streets / city / environment signatures identical, `drive-edge-test` and the garage tests as before (T2 log).
* Files: `src/world/landmarksTauride/{height.js, terrain-mesh.js, data/relief.js}`, `groups.js`, `src/world/landmarksTauride.js` (the entry builds the terrain and publishes `terrain: { heightAt, triangles, draws }`), `hooks/{height,keep-out}.js`, `hooks.js`, `lib/area-tables.js` (the known key `carKeepOut`), `drive/handling.js`, `props/osm.js`, `people/nav.js`, the golden `tools/golden/layout-north.json`; tools `tauride-relief-plan.mjs`, `tauride/{relief-check, people-probe, heightat-bench, strip-furniture-claims, car-keepout-sim, terrain-material-test}.mjs`, `tauride/lib/relief-rules.mjs`; tests: `north-hooks-test` (200 checks), `north-height-test` (324), `tauride-height-test --model north --build landmarksTauride --assert-cars` (60 relief checks, 15 of them mutants).

### 8.11 T3 of the Tauride remake: the garden floor, the painted ground, the draped alleys

Phase T3 of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (its log, section 12, has the commits, the numbers and the commands). Branch `tb-t3`. CPU only; the one look at the result is a software-GL render (`tools/tauride/ground-preview.mjs`, SwiftShader: no GPU), the GPU QA is the next step.

* **Decision (with evidence): the terrain mesh covers the WHOLE garden polygon, and the environment ground is cut out under it.** The relief lifts 12,208 m2 of the 197,570 m2 of the garden by 2 cm or more (6.2 %): 93.8 % of the garden is flat ground, and the stripes of the open meadows (66,454 m2 in the mask), the leaf litter under the woods (88,259 m2, 44.7 %), the shore and the worn earth are painted on exactly that ground, so a floor that covered only the hills could not carry them (the alternative, matching the lawn colour of the environment ground at the foot of each hill, leaves the lawn between the hills unpaintable). It also makes T4 simpler: the pond beds and banks are cut and built in the module's own floor, no engine hook for the ground. Cost: 5,479 -> 9,080 triangles (+3,601: the flat leaves of 16 m and the clipped border), still 16 draws; the environment ground loses the 19.8 ha (its fan triangles become cell pieces at the hole: +1,594 triangles in `check:north`).
* **The floor** (`terrain-mesh.js`, cover mode, `planTerrain(gh, box, { cover: gardenPieces() })`): the T2 quadtree with every leaf that touches the polygon kept (flat ground = one 16 m leaf = 2 triangles) and the leaves across the outline clipped to it EXACTLY: the outline is the subject and the triangle the clip of a Sutherland-Hodgman pass (no internal diagonal, which would crack a neighbour that lies wholly inside), the cut points canonical (`environment/clip.js clipHalf`) and snapped to 1/4096 m, a notch is earcut (checked against the area, a fallback to the convex pieces never ran), and the lattice is shifted by a dyadic 152/4096, 211/4096 m so that no outline vertex (OSM rounds to 0.1 m) lies exactly on a lattice line (without the shift 6 edges are open inside the polygon: a mutant of the test). Measured on the real outline: the triangles tile the polygon (197,570.0 m2 = the polygon, 0 of 790,327 lattice points uncovered, 0 triangles outside, no corner more than 0.2 mm outside), watertight in the exact float32 positions (0 open edges inside, 0 in more than two triangles), within 4.45 cm of `gardenHeight` (the 5 cm check), `heightAt` is untouched. `makeMeshHeight(plan)` answers the height of the PLANNED mesh (8 m buckets, barycentric): what the alleys are draped on.
* **The seam with the environment ground** (`HOOKS.groundHoles`, 9.1): the hole in the environment ground is the outline moved in by 6 cm (`hooks/ground.js insetRing`), so the floor overlaps the ground by 6 cm all round and no float32 crack between the two meshes can show through; in that band the floor wins by geometry (it is drawn 5 mm higher, `TERRAIN.lift`; both have polygonOffset 2, as the ground always had, so everything that sits above the ground at 0.02 .. 0.12 m keeps winning over the floor too). The environment ground under the floor is not shaded at all. **There is no seam with the Palace court or the Paradny podium to show: the Palace footprint is 18.5 m and the podium 69.4 m from the outline** (`ground-test`); what meets the floor is the environment ground and the streets' pavements at the fence line.
* **The ground mask** (`ground-material.js planGroundMask`, pure and deterministic, nothing committed as a binary): RGBA8 at 1 m over the garden box (587 x 536 cells, **1,258,528 bytes**, 85-170 ms): R the canopy weight (the 23 woods with a 6 m feather, plus the authored belt of `data/ground.js CANOPY_BELT`), G the signed distance to the water outline (128 = the waterline, 12 levels per metre: the muddy shore band, the 0.5 m wet band for T4 and, until T4 cuts the ponds, the bed under the future water), B the wear (benches, gates, junctions of three ways, the monuments and the fountain, the authored plazas, the verge of every path, the apron of a building, the playgrounds), A the mown-stripe code (block << 5 | weight: the nearest authored meadow block times the openness of the lawn, no stripe under a canopy, on a path, on a steep slope or within 2.5 m of the water; read NEAREST with `texelFetch`). Inputs: `data/ground.js` (authored: 5 meadow blocks, the belt, 4 worn plazas, the playground ids, the tuning), the relief (`gardenHeight`: the slope cut of the stripes), `data/water.js`, the facade GREEN woods, `data/paths.js`, `data/furniture.js`, `data/footprints.js`.
* **The material** (`gardenGroundMaterial`): a second `ctx.mat.toon` with its own `onBeforeCompile` (the pattern of `environment/ground.js`): ONE category per pixel from noise fields with cel-hard edges and no repeating texture (a 128^2 noise tile read at six scales in a warped domain so that its 2.7 m lattice does not show), the ground's own four lawn greens plus clover patches and mown stripes, litter under the canopy, mud / wet band / bed at the shore, worn and packed earth, and the relief READ FROM ABOVE from the vertex normal and the height (darker and greener flanks, a lit side and a shade side from the REAL sun (`layout.SUN_DIR`: the south-west; the first version used a painter's light from the north-west), drier crowns, a contour tone; from 90 to 280 m the soft term turns into three cel tones, T3 fix round 2), the far fog of the environment (`FAR_FOG`) and the melt to the mean colour at distance. polygonOffset +2, no vertex colour. The floor meshes are `ctx.noBatch` and, since T3 fix round 1, IN the normal / depth pre-pass like the near chunks of the environment ground they replace (the first version had them `noOutline`, the QA found the outline pass seeing through the floor, see the fix-round bullet below); the ribbons and stairs stay colour-only.
* **The alleys** (`paths-draped.js`): the 106 non-bridge, non-steps ways as ONE ribbon mesh (one draw): corner-cut with the ends and every shared vertex pinned, rows of three vertices (five where the terrain curves across the ribbon, 12 ways) at a spacing that adapts to the planned mesh (a row where the chord is more than 1.4 cm off it, 1 .. 8 m), every vertex the planned mesh + 3 cm (+ 4 / 2 mm for main / side, so a join has a winner), polygonOffset -1.4 like the streets' footways; width +-8 % along the way, the shader cuts a ragged edge (+-0.17 m of a noise of the world position) and paints a darker worn centre line and lighter edges that fade out within 2.5 m of a junction, so two ribbons that overlap there show the same colour; an 8-triangle disc under each of the 106 junctions; gravel `#c9a98a` with tones down to `#b58c78`, earth trails `#917658`, paving stones and concrete plates `#aaa7a1`. No kerbs on the ribbons. **The 8 bridge ways are not drawn (T4).** The 6 steps ways are real STAIRS (a second mesh, one draw): n = round(drop / 0.15) treads between the two ends, never under the mesh, risers to the mesh, a kerb 0.2 m wide on both sides; the two stairs of the wooden bridge rise 0.6 m (4 x 0.15) towards the deck: **the deck of T4 must stand 0.6 m above the lawn at the bridge ends**. The ways follow the terrain: P50 / P90 / max offset of the ribbon centrelines from the data ways 0.000 / 0.000 / 0.47 m (circular: the model against its own data; the overlay against an image is T7). 11,276 triangles (budget 12,000), 2 draws (budget 4), 26 ms.
* **The claimed ways** (`HOOKS.groundWayReject`, 9.1): the class mask of the environment no longer paints the grey gravel / paving of the 120 garden ways (the mean gravel weight on the centrelines is 0.077, the park lawn's, 0.82 with the hook off); the service lane `w1258039516 / 17` is NOT claimed: streets draws it as the asphalt strip it is, inside the garden, cars cannot drive on it (the keep-out).
* **Fix round 1 (the QA of T3, docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md section 12)**: (1) the floor is back in the outline pass (`buildTerrain`: `opts.noOutline ?? false`): out of it the pass drew the outlines of whatever stands behind a hill over the hill's face and ended the environment ground (in the pass) against the floor (not) in a two-pixel line along the whole garden outline; with it, hidden-box ghost pixels on a hill face 68 -> 0 and the outline line 1,920 -> 0 pixels in the engine's real render pipeline on software GL (`tools/tauride/outline-preview.mjs`); (2) the four footway AREAS inside the fence are claimed (`groups.js GARDEN_AREAS`), which removes the kerb wall of 27 m radius at the central round; (3) the dry-leaf flecks of the ground shader no longer repeat with the noise tile (jittered fine field and a second, turned read: correlation at the tile lag 1.000 -> 0.043); (4) the relief term follows the gradient of the height and multiplies after the litter (lit / shade 1.13 -> 1.83 on a 1:7 flank), the litter is 0.80 of the lawn's lightness (was 0.69) and starts inside a wood, the belt is a shade fringe (`LOOK`, `GROUND_COLORS`, `CANOPY_BELT`). The numbers are DESIGN values and a software-GL look, not a GPU one.
* **Fix round 2 (the GPU QA of fix round 1: "hills do not read from above": a lit dome and a shade flank at <= 120 m, only soft dark smudges at 250-480 m)**: verified on software GL (the same shader, `ground-preview.mjs`, the QA's poses): the smooth term (amplitude 0.42, gain 5, melted away with the patchwork from 160 m) leaves a lit side that is no brighter than the lawn's own lighter blotches, and the light came from the north-west although the sun, and so every shadow of the buildings, is in the south-west. The shader now (1) takes the light from the real sun (`LOOK.sun` = `layout.SUN_DIR` on the ground plane, the uniform follows `ctx.sunDir`), (2) from `farA` 90 m to `farB` 280 m turns the soft term into three cel tones (shade / flat / lit, the engine's own light-and-shadow split of `makeGradientMap`: a share `cel` 0.45 of the step, gain 5 -> 10, amplitude 0.42 -> 0.40) with a warm lit and a cool shade side and a lifted crown, (3) melts the lawn's patchwork BEFORE the relief is applied (the relief no longer melts with it) and calms it from 100 m (`meltA`), (4) draws the contour bands at any distance while one band is at least ~6 px wide (`fwidth`, not the distance of the fine detail). Near range (<= 90 m) is unchanged but for the light's direction. Measured with the port of the term on the nine real hills (`tools/tauride/hill-legibility.mjs --old`): the lit half over the shade half 1.34-1.67 (first version) -> 2.25-2.32 at 300 and 480 m, the share of a hill that differs from flat ground by 12 % or more 49-73 % -> 70-88 %; on the software-GL renders (top-down over a hill, relief divided by the same view with the relief off) the relief's lit / shade tone ratio 1.4-1.6 -> 2.1-2.2 and its size over the lawn's own noise (lit minus shade tone divided by the relative spread of the flat lawn) 1.6-2.1 -> 3.9-5.2 at 300-480 m. The memorial tip with its 1.2 m plateau is the least visible (70 %). A GPU look is still the QA's.
* **Proof that the strip is untouched**: both engine call sites read their hook through `lib/area-tables.js` behind a falsy test and the strip exports `HOOKS = {}`; `layout-golden --diff` (strip) identical, the strip's `npm run check` identical in all 25 modules, the strip streets / city / environment signatures identical, `drive-edge-test`, `drive-collision-test` and the garage tests as before (T3 log).
* Files: `src/world/landmarksTauride/{terrain-mesh.js, ground-material.js, paths-draped.js, data/ground.js}`, `src/world/landmarksTauride.js`, `src/areas/north/hooks/ground.js`, `hooks.js`, `lib/area-tables.js` (the known keys), `environment/ground.js`, `environment/field.js`; tools `tauride/{ground-test, ground-preview, ground-bench}.mjs` (`terrain-material-test.mjs` of T2 is replaced); tests: `ground-test` (85 checks since fix round 2, 78 after fix round 1, 64 before), `outline-preview` (software GL, the outline pass), `north-hooks-test` (202), `tauride-height-test --model north --build landmarksTauride --assert-cars` (60 relief checks).

### 8.12 T4a of the Tauride remake: the ponds - bowls, banks, water, (impassable shores: reversed by 13.7), water mask

Phase T4a of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (its log, section 12, has the commits, the numbers and the commands). Branch `tb-t4a`. CPU only, no GPU frame. The bridges, the dam, the reeds and the ducks are T4b: the 8 bridge ways stay claimed and not drawn, so the water is a closed barrier until the decks exist.

* **One datum table** (`landmarksTauride/data/ponds.js`, pure data): water level -0.45, beds -1.2 (Big Pond: the lake r1582714 with the east and the south arm) and -0.5 (Small Pond: the west pond r6278222 and its canal to the dam; 5 cm of water), crest +0.1, bank slope 1:3 .. 1:5 at the waterline (a slow product of two sines of the position), the collider contour -0.3 and top 1.8, the wet band 0.5 m, the bed colours. The water surface, the bowls, the banks, the colliders, the ground material and the tests are all DERIVED from it (a mutant of the table with one number changed turns a test red: `tools/tauride/pond-test.mjs`, 12 mutants). The old module's five disagreeing datums (water -0.55, ground cut -0.4, bed -1.4, shore box up to +0.22) are gone.
* **The terrain** (`landmarksTauride/height.js`): `gardenHeight` is now the TERRAIN = the pond bowls and banks + the relief (`reliefHeight` is the pure relief, the tools and the mutants of the T2 rules use it). The shore is the signed distance to the union outline (`makeShore`: a 2 m cell grid, the cells within the reach of the profile (8.5 m land, 7.5 m water) hold the list of the segments near them (CSR arrays, 28 k entries); the distance is the nearest segment, the SIGN comes from the pseudo-normal of the nearest segment or vertex, which is exact for a simple polygon; compared with a brute-force even-odd test on 40,000 random points it differs by 4e-15; 444 KB, built once at module load in about 40 ms). Profile: in the water `h = WATER_Y - u / s` to the bed with a quadratic knee (`BED_KNEE`), on land a quadratic ease `h = WATER_Y + (BANK_TOP - WATER_Y) (1 - (1 - d / D)^2)` up to the crest at `D = 2 (BANK_TOP - WATER_Y) s` (the slope at the waterline is exactly 1 : s, so the two sides meet without a kink) and the crest falls to the lawn over `CREST_FALL` 3 m; the relief is ADDED on land (it is zero within 3 m of the water by its own rules). Beyond the reach the terrain IS the relief, bit for bit (44,944 of 44,944 points). **Gated by the garden polygon**: the lake's north shore coincides with the garden outline for 170 m (11 stretches of the union outline, measured in fix round 1; the fence stands on the shore): there the land side is the street (flat, y = 0, drawn by the environment) and the step from the street to the water is a quay of 0.45 m, not a bank (`makeShore` stores per cell whether the cell is inside, outside or crossed by the outline; within 5 cm of the outline counts as inside). The HEIGHT_PATCH `garden` has the box `TERRAIN_HULL` (the hills and the shore band: 441 x 459 m).
* **The floor** (`terrain-mesh.js`, unchanged but for the flat test of a leaf, which no longer calls a bowl flat, and the defaults, which are the pure relief for the T2 relief-only mode): the cover plan over `gardenHeight` was 32.1 k triangles in 16 draws (T3: 9,080), **22.6 k after fix round 1** (`floorOptions()`: split 0.06 above the water, a hidden bed under it, a corner rule; +13.5 k over T3, the gate was +15 k for the floor and the water together): above the water within 5 cm of `gardenHeight` for 99.90 % of the samples of a 0.5 m lattice (mean 0.27 cm), 10 cm at the worst (the cusps of the canals: the smallest leaf is 1 m); the bed under the opaque water is off by at most 25 cm (`FLOOR_TUNING.sub`) and never above the surface by more than the base tolerance. The cost is inherent to a quadtree: the profile has a curvature of 0.04 .. 0.15 per m2 along 2.7 km of shore, so a ±4 m band of 1 m leaves; a strip mesh along the shore would be about half of it and is a larger change.
* **The water** (`landmarksTauride/ponds.js`): one mesh of 201 triangles (earcut over the union with its 3 holes: 29,669 m2, the data's net area) at y -0.45, `waterMaterial()` of `environment/water.js` with the ONE new LOOK key `'tauride-pond'` (greenish, turbid, olive at the banks), `noBatch`, `noOutline`, polygonOffset (the Small Pond is 5 cm deep) and a duckweed mat patched into the fragment shader (green flecks within 6 m of the bank in the Small Pond: it reads the distance atlas of the field, which knows the ponds through `HOOKS.waterBodies`). **The kerb skirt (the quay; fix round 1, generalised in fix round 2)**: the environment ground (street, y 0) is cut at the garden outline and the floor stops there, so wherever the floor at the outline is not at the street level there is a step that nothing closed (the GPU QA saw the sky through it as a pale blue slit: from the lake, across the canal at T05, at the dam channel and at the ends of the quay). Fix round 1 closed only the 170 m where the WATER lies on the outline; the step is 0.45 m there, but it also exists where the bank has only partly risen to the street (the water 1 .. 3 m off the outline: 22 .. 41 cm steps in four runs, 48.5 m) and where the crest (+0.1) stands above it (35 m). `ponds.js planQuay(floor, ring)` now walks the open edges of the floor plan that lie on the garden outline (the plan has the exact terrain height at every vertex, cut points included, and is linear between them) and stitches a vertical wall from each edge to the street level: a floor BELOW the street gets a wall facing the garden (the viewer on the water), a floor ABOVE it one facing the street; steps of 2 cm or less get none; an edge that crosses the street level is cut there; consecutive edges within 3 mm of their chord become one quad; the wall reaches 3 cm past the surface it meets. `data/ponds.js QUAY` is the table (`drop`, `under`, `merge`, `tol`, colour). Granite grey, one more draw: 124 triangles in 62 quads over 265.5 m of the outline (223.5 m below the street, 42 m above it), the pond service says 2 draws, 325 triangles; planned in 16 ms from the floor plan (`planPonds({ floor })`; without a floor plan there is no skirt).
* **[Reversed by the water rule of 2026-10-25, section 13.7: the shore colliders are no longer built and the landing is never refused; the text below is the history of T4a.]** **Impassable shores**: 195 boxes (2,655 m) along the contour of the bank at -0.3 m, inland of the shore, top 1.8 m (`physics.js` blocks a walker whose feet + 0.45 is below the top, at every feet height down to the bottom -2.5 m: a jump from the crest, apex +0.73, cannot clear it). A ring of 782 probe starts every 3 m along the shore, 7 angles (0, ±30, ±60, ±80 degrees from the perpendicular) x walk / sprint / jump, on the real `Player` + `Physics` of the built module: 0 of 16,422 runs enter the water, the closest feet come to 1.2 m from the waterline. The harness can fail: no colliders -> 120 of 120 runs wade in; the old module's 0.22 m box on the crest -> 300 of 300 enter, 122 cannot get out; a top below contour + 0.45 -> 120 of 120 enter. `ponds.js planPonds({ gaps })` is the hook for the bridge heads of T4b.
* **The water mask** (`HOOKS.waterBodies`, 9.1): `env.waterAt` is true in the ponds and false on the land around them (20,000 random points, 0 wrong outside 1.2 m of the outline). Pedestrians: `nav.blocked` reads `env.waterAt`; `nav.surfaceY` also stands a walker on a garden bank below the street (th < -0.06, inside the garden), as T2 did for the hills.
* **[Reversed by the water rule of 2026-10-25, section 13.7: the shore colliders are no longer built and the landing is never refused; the text below is the history of T4a.]** **Flight landing (fix round 1)**: the engine refused a landing only where the ground is below -1 (`player.js landingBlocked`: the Big Pond deeper than 0.55 m); in the shallows and the whole Small Pond (-0.5) a flyer landed inside the ring of shore colliders and could not walk out (9 of 9 landings of the QA). `HOOKS.noLanding` (9.1; `hooks/water.js makeNoLanding`) refuses the ponds as 'water': true where the terrain is at or below the contour of the colliders (-0.3). `core/player.js` has the field `noLanding` (null = the old test only) and one line in `landingBlocked`; `main.js` hands it `HOOKS.noLanding`. On the real Player: 18 spots over the water, all refused; without the hook 15 of 15 landings in the shallows are accepted and trapped; 10 lawn spots stay landable. (The 8 bridge ways crossed a closed shore until T4b opened it at the bridge heads: 8.13.)
* **The banks (fix round 1)**: the GPU QA read the bank as one flat dark olive-brown strip with a ruled edge. The ground shader (`ground-material.js`) now makes damp turf of it (the grass darkened and yellowed toward the water over ~3 m), mud in blotches at the waterline (a threshold of the clump fields, never a continuous band), patchy sand, a soft wet multiply, lighter bed colours (`BED_COLORS`), edges jittered by the slow fields, and less flank shade within 5 m of the water. Software-GL stand-in views: `D:/git2/n4spb-work/tauride/T4a/fix1/preview-before|after`.
* **The cost of `heightAt` at start-up** is NOT within the +30 % gate over the Paradny-only baseline: +66 % cold (about +12 ms once, +1.5 ms warm); the terrain patch box is 97 % of the garden box (the shore band of the lake and the canals spans it). See the T4a fix-round log entry: a decision for the lead (accept, or re-base the gate).
* **Proof that the strip is untouched**: the one engine call site (`field.js`) reads its hook through `lib/area-tables.js` behind a falsy test; the strip's field raster is the same bytes (sha1 on a 4 m lattice of 12 quantities), `layout-golden --diff` (strip) identical, the strip's `npm run check` identical (see the log).
* Files (fix round 1 added `src/core/player.js`, `src/main.js`, `ponds.js planQuay`, `terrain-mesh.js floorOptions`): `src/world/landmarksTauride/{data/ponds.js, ponds.js, height.js, terrain-mesh.js, ground-material.js}`, `src/world/landmarksTauride.js`, `src/world/environment/{water.js, field.js}`, `src/world/people/nav.js`, `src/world/lib/area-tables.js`, `src/areas/north/{hooks.js, hooks/water.js, hooks/height.js}`; tools `tauride/{pond-test, pond-people-probe, field-dump}.mjs` (61 checks / 12 datum mutants + the skirt mutants and a ray test of the outline; the pedestrians at the ponds; the field rasters), `tauride-height-test.mjs` (section PONDS: the ring, 16,422 runs), `tauride/lib/height-harness.mjs wadeRing`; tests: `north-hooks-test` (203), `north-keepout-test` (18), `ground-test` (85).

### 8.13 T4b of the Tauride remake: the bridges, the dam, the reeds, the duck lanes, the pedestrians on the decks

Phase T4b of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (its log, section 12, has the bridge table as built, the decisions, the numbers and the commands). Branch `tb-t4b`. CPU only, no GPU frame.

* **The kit** (`landmarksTauride/bridges.js`, the design values in `data/bridge-design.js`, the mapped facts in `data/bridges.js`): three types (iron arch No.3 / No.4 with granite abutments, marble terminal posts, a cast-iron balustrade and iron ribs; humped wooden No.1 with planks by colour, wooden rails and the T3 stairs at both heads; flat metal No.2 / No.5) and the dam (fix round 1: a warm light granite body across the channel standing 0.45 m proud of the water on both faces, the dark walkway on its crest at the street level, a coping 0.15 m above the deck line on the SPILL face with a translucent nappe hanging from its lip to the water and a foam strip with a wavy edge: ONE water level, the T4a datum; fix round 2: the faces come from the data, `DAM.mouthOnLake` is SOUTH of the dam, so the spill face with the sill is the north = Small Pond face (`bridge-design.js DAM.spillToLake` false) and the plain body with `bodyOut` faces the lake), on the six crossing sites; 8,354 triangles in 2 draws (budget 10,000 / 2). The AXIS of a site is the data's deck extended to dry land past the shore colliders or to the stair feet; **`deckY(x, z)` is the walking profile along the whole axis** (ramp or stair, deck, far ramp; a table of knots, bbox-first, pure, no allocation, 2.8 ms for the garden lattice), the head of every deck is the ribbon surface of the alley exactly. The player walks the same profile: 92 sticky walk ramps and 28 rail boxes (1.3 m above the deck: a hop cannot clear them; the ramps run under the rails).
* **The shore ring opens onto the decks only** (`ponds.js planPonds({ decks })`, `chordOutside`: the T4a chords are cut where a deck rectangle crosses them, 15 chords, 48.4 m; 195 -> 204 boxes); the wading ring of T4a still finds 0 entries, and the new DECK RING (`lib/height-harness.mjs deckRing`: 5,691 runs from starts on every deck and at every head, walk / sprint / jump) 0 entries; the south gate -> memorial route ARRIVES on foot over bridge No.4 (`tauride-height-test` section BRIDGES).
* **The pedestrians** (9.1 `HOOKS.deckServices`, three strip-neutral hooks in `people/nav.js` behind the same table): a point on a service deck is not water, `surfaceY` adds the service's `deckLift` (0.02 on a garden bridge; the podium keeps 0.12), and the service's `walkways` (the deck axes) become footway nodes and edges (the mapped bridge ways are not in `streets.paths`: `streets/paths.js` skips `r.bridge`, so without it no edge of the graph crossed the water). 42 nav nodes on the decks at `deckY + 0.02`, 235 walker samples on the decks in 5 minutes (144 of them over the water, p50 0.03 m), 0 in the water (`pond-people-probe`; fix round 1: the probe pins the CROSSING, a route of the graph from axis end to axis end over the water on every site, so the water exemption of `nav.js` is a mutant it catches).
* **The reeds** (`reeds.js`): 500 instanced clumps (3,500 triangles, 1 draw) in belts along the shore band (a slow noise of the arc length: 133 belts over 806 m of the 2,666 m shore), none at the alley ends, the alleys, the stairs, the decks, the dam or the garden outline (the quay and the skirt stretches).
* **The ducks** (9.1 `HOOKS.duckPonds`, `hooks/water.js makeDuckPonds`, the pond branch of `wildlife/ducks.js`): 15 lanes at the water level -0.45 on the shore rings (water on the left of travel), every sample water for the field and 3 m off every shore, none under a deck; 5 groups, 19 ducks; the strip's ducks are the same (`wildlife-test` strip and North OK; T5 note: `wildlife-test` EXEMPTS the key `'pond'` from its river check, so it proves only that the pond lanes do not break the test; that the lanes and the groups stay on the pond water is proved by `bridge-test --ducks`).
* **The narrow arms** (the T4a GPU QA's pale speckled gravel): the LOOK `'tauride-pond'` darker and more teal with a calmer chop, and `ponds.js pondMaterial` damps the shallow tint band (2.4 m), the ripple iso-lines (x 0.4) and the sun glints (x 0.3) through `data/ponds.js POND_LOOK`; the decks are deck zones of the water (a soffit shadow kept to the deck). Fix round 1 (the east aerials with the sun ahead still showed a pale speckled band): the engine's low-sun glitter sheet is halved (`sunSheet`) and both sun terms fade out with the view elevation (`air`: gone when the camera looks down at more than about 30 degrees; at eye level the sun's path on the water stays). `tools/tauride/bridge-preview.mjs` shows the pair on software GL.
* **The alleys** (`paths-draped.js`, the bank-zone pass): a row is five wide only where it needs it, rows of different width are zipped, folded triangles are dropped (the T4a slivers): 11,084 + 524 = 11,608 triangles, back under the T3 budget of 12,000 (T4a 14,252).
* **Proof that the strip is untouched**: `layout-golden --diff` identical (strip and North: `heightAt` is untouched, `deckY` is a service), the strip's `npm run check` identical in all 25 modules, the streets / city / environment signatures identical, the strip's field raster the same bytes (sha1 6435bf0e...), the North field raster the same bytes as T4a (sha1 d2f0f182...), `drive-edge-test` 960 runs / 1,556 hits / 0 stuck, `wildlife-test` strip OK; the four engine files touched (`people/nav.js`, `wildlife/ducks.js`, `lib/area-tables.js` (the key `duckPonds`), `environment/water.js` (the LOOK entry)) do nothing without the area's tables.
* Files: `src/world/landmarksTauride/{bridges.js, reeds.js, data/bridge-design.js}` (new), `src/world/landmarksTauride/{ponds.js, paths-draped.js, data/ponds.js}`, `src/world/landmarksTauride.js`, `src/world/environment/water.js`, `src/world/people/nav.js`, `src/world/wildlife/ducks.js`, `src/world/lib/area-tables.js`, `src/areas/north/{hooks.js, hooks/water.js}`; tools `tauride/bridge-test.mjs` (new, 47 checks), `tauride-height-test.mjs` (section BRIDGES), `tauride/lib/height-harness.mjs` (`deckRing`, the deck-aware `wadeRing` / `depthAt`), `tauride/{pond-test, ground-test, pond-people-probe, heightat-bench}.mjs`, `north-hooks-test.mjs` (206), `north-keepout-test.mjs` (18).

### 8.14 T5 of the Tauride remake: the garden's own trees

Phase T5 of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (its log, section 12, has the zones as built, the species table, the counts per tier, the numbers and the commands). Branch `tb-t5`. CPU only, no GPU frame.

* **The planting** (`landmarksTauride/vegetation.js planVegetation`, the authored zones, species mixes, keep-outs, clearings and budgets in `data/vegetation.js`; pure and deterministic, a keyed hash per zone): the border belt of old trees in the band 1.5 .. 14 m inside the fence (a jittered lattice, oak 40 / lime 30 / larch 10 / ash 10 / maple 10, dissolved 9 m round the 11 gates and the 13 fence openings), lime avenues on both sides of the 21 main alleys (the trunks 2.75 m off the ribbon edge, 7.5 .. 9.5 m apart), groves on the 23 OSM wood polygons (a 9 m lattice thinned by a slow noise into clearings and knots, 12 % shrubs), five solitary old oaks on the mown meadows with a 15 m clearing each, the shore planting (alder, birch, shrubs) on the CREST band of the banks (the bank profile of `data/ponds.js`: never on the slope), and the mapped OSM trees as old oaks (scale 1.2 .. 1.4) at their nodes, every keep-out applied (fix round 2: a node that a rule refuses is nudged to the nearest clear spot within `ZONES.osm.nudge` 4 m, rings of 0.25 m x 24 directions, or refused: 6 of the 7 planted, 4 nudged 0.5 .. 2.5 m, the node wedged between the perimeter alley w425201986 and the trail w112881682 refused; `tree.node` records the move). Zones in the order osm, meadow, belt, avenue, shore, grove (the packing couples a tree to the ones placed before it); the species draw is tilted towards the lagging shares, so the zone mixes hold within 5 points. The tier: 996 instances at medium (938 trees + 58 shrubs), 681 at performance, a SUBSET chosen by each tree's own hash (`BUDGET`).
* **The keep-outs** (`KEEP_OUT`, each asserted with a mutant by `tools/tauride/vegetation-test.mjs`): the 13 building footprints + 3 m, every way ribbon + 1.5 m to the trunk surface and NO leaf lower than 2.5 m over a ribbon (`GARDEN_SPECIES.base` = the lowest leaf of the family's LOD0 canopy, pinned to the real geometry: a larch or a shrub keeps its whole radius off the alleys), the water and the bank slope (the crest + 1 m for every zone but the shore), the bridge decks + 3 m, the 12 bridge heads + 5 m, the dam + 5 m, the 6 stairs + 4 m, every reed clump + 1 m, the camera poses + 4 m and a 30 m look corridor (`CLEARINGS`: the 11 ground poses of `tools/tauride-cams.mjs` and the two garden VIEWS, pinned to those files), the pitch / playgrounds / fitness station + 3 m, the fountain 14 m, the monuments 8 m, the memorial plateau M + 2 m, the gates and openings 9 m, the service lane + 1.5 m, the outline 1.5 m, slopes over 0.18 for the old species (0.24 for all), the crown packing 0.55.
* **The build** (`buildVegetation`): the ENGINE's trees: `treegeo.js canopyGeometry / trunkGeometry` (one `InstancedMesh` per family x 4 canopy LODs + 2 trunk LODs, 58 meshes for 10 families), the shared `env-canopy` / `env-conifer` / `env-bark-pb-*` materials of `leafmat.js` (the same material and texture objects as the environment's trees: the caches), the instance writer of `tree-instances.js` with a `shapeOf` that puts every tree at `gardenHeight(x, z)` (the banks and the hills), the LOD distances 42 / 150 / 360 with the environment's 10 % hysteresis (no view cone: the garden is small), contact-shadow blobs on flat ground only, a trunk cylinder per tree from the street level (lower on a bank) to y + 4.5 m (`people/nav.js` reads it as an obstacle: `tree-people-probe`). The larch is a garden-local species row (`SPECIES_EXTRA`; fix round 1: a narrow `tiers` cone H 21 / R 2.4 on a clear stem of 3.6 m with its underside, through the optional row keys `y0` / `under` / `stem` of `treegeo.js addTiers` and the tiers trunk, whose defaults are the engine's constants: all 16 engine families bit-identical; was an open tent H 20 / R 3.4 from 0.9 m up) through the new optional third parameter of `canopyGeometry(fam, lod, F = SPECIES[fam])` / `trunkGeometry` (a default parameter: the strip's calls are unchanged, the strip oracles identical). Triangles: 19,820 at the HERO (every tree at LOD3: what `check:north` counts), 254,199 in the middle of the garden (the engine's own per-instance cost: an old crown is ~1,800 triangles at LOD0); draws 11 / 52.
* **The ground follows the planting** (`ground-material.js planGroundMask` `canopy`): the canopy class of the mask is stamped from the PLANTED crowns (a disc per tree, 0.5 at the crown edge), not from the OSM woods and the authored belt (those remain the fallback of a mask planned without a planting; `data/ground.js CANOPY_BELT` width 8 -> 12 to match the belt as planted). 100 % of the litter cells lie under a crown (the T3 mask: 36 %), the litter is 16.3 % of the garden (the OSM woods were 44.7 %). The entry now plans everything first (the floor, the bridges, the alleys, the ponds, the reeds, the trees) and builds the meshes after the mask.
* **The hook**: `HOOKS.treeReject` (9.1 H03) answers `'garden'` inside the garden outline when `landmarksTauride` is built: the 7 mapped OSM trees of the facade (no ids to claim) are no longer planted by the environment (`env.treeStats.rej['osm:garden']` 7, 0 environment trunks inside the outline; `north-env-snapshot`: the strip identical, the North's changes inside the garden box). The strip exports no `HOOKS`.
* **Fix round 1** (the reviewers; the log entry "T5, fix round 1" of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md): the T11 pose was screened not by the garden but by the environment's planting of the OSM wood w1274934137 on the palace side (the lake's north shore is the outline; the facade stands 20 .. 35 m north of it): `HOOKS.treeCleared` (9.1 H02) now clears the palace parterre `PALACE_PARTERRE` with `landmarksTauride` built (a design decision for the lead to confirm; one data row); the larch redone (above); the zone geometry pinned on the built list with a mutant each; `vegetation-test` 89 checks, `north-hooks-test` 208, `north-keepout-test` 18.
* **Wildlife**: the module publishes `services.landmarksTauride.treeList` in the shape of `environment.trees`; `wildlife/world.js` reads the tree lists of the landmark gardens from a hard-coded list (`landmarksE / F / H / J`), so the crows' perches and the ground birds' trunk grid do not see the garden's trees until that line names `landmarksTauride` (outside T5's files: for the lead). `wildlife-test` North and strip OK.
* **The T4b leftovers**: the reeds (`reeds.js`, `REEDS`: one geometry of three sub-clumps of different heights, 13 blades, a non-uniform scale per instance, two rows (the shallows -1.1 .. -0.32 m and the wet foot to +0.35, 55 % in the shallows), denser belts (spacing 0.7, threshold 0.58), 700 instances, 9,100 triangles, still 1 draw); `heightat-bench.mjs --repeat 25` gates the RATIO full / paradny of the same run (<= 1.10 x the T4a ratio 1.67 = 1.84; the absolute line stays); `bridge-test` pins `HEAD_LIFT` == `PATHS_DRAPED.offset`, `pond-test` pins `QUAY.under` (a skirt with `under: 0` is red), `north-people-hooks-test` pins the garden branch of `nav.surfaceY` (the text, and in `--scene` 100 bank points at the terrain + 0.02); the T11 pose moved to (1442, -1556) yaw 35.5 (the palace front across the lake; `tauride-cams-check --colliders` and `views-check` green); `.playwright-mcp/` in `.gitignore`.
* **Proof that the strip is untouched**: `layout-golden --diff` identical, the strip's `npm run check` identical in 25 modules, the streets / city / environment signatures identical, the strip environment snapshot identical, the strip field raster the same bytes (sha1 6435bf0e...), `drive-edge-test` 960 runs / 1,556 hits / 0 stuck, `wildlife-test` strip OK; the two engine files touched (`treegeo.js`: a default parameter; `hooks/keep-out.js`: a North hook body) do nothing without the area's tables.
* Files (fix round 1 adds `src/areas/north/hooks/keep-out.js` (`PALACE_PARTERRE`, `treeCleared`), `src/world/environment/treegeo.js` (the tiers row keys), `tools/north-hooks-test.mjs`, `tools/north-keepout-test.mjs`): `src/world/landmarksTauride/{vegetation.js, data/vegetation.js}` (new), `src/world/landmarksTauride/{ground-material.js, reeds.js, data/ground.js, data/bridge-design.js}`, `src/world/landmarksTauride.js`, `src/world/environment/treegeo.js`, `src/areas/north/{hooks.js, hooks/keep-out.js}`, `tools/check.mjs` (BUDGET 700 k -> 765 k: the measured 695,273 + 10 %), `tools/tauride-cams.mjs` (T11), `.gitignore`; tools `tauride/vegetation-test.mjs` (new, 72 checks), `tauride/tree-people-probe.mjs` (new), `tauride/{bridge-test, pond-test, ground-test, heightat-bench}.mjs`, `tauride-height-test.mjs`, `north-hooks-test.mjs` (207), `north-keepout-test.mjs`, `north-people-hooks-test.mjs`.

### 8.15 T6 of the Tauride remake: the fence and gates, the furniture, the lanterns, the monuments

Phase T6 of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (its log, section 12, has the fence table, the numbers, the decisions and the commands). Branch `tb-t6`. CPU only, no GPU frame; one software-GL render (`tools/tauride/furniture-preview.mjs`, 41 views) as a stand-in for the look.

* **The fence and the gates** (`landmarksTauride/fence.js`): `planFence` (pure) cuts every segment of the 14 mapped fence ways into `ceil(len / 2.7)` equal panels (the rule of the old `garden.js`: 932 panels; the data's `FENCE_STATS.panels` 897 is `ceil(way length / 2.7)` per way), marks the panels of the 13 `FENCE_OPENINGS` open (each cluster's first and last centre are panel centres of the layout to the millimetre: 38 open panels) and leaves the 4 `FENCE_GAPS` unfenced; a panel is a granite plinth (-0.3 .. 0.22 m) and an alpha-cut picket plane (spears, two rails, bulbs: the strip's picket pattern, one 512 x 512 atlas shared with the opaque bands of the piers and the diamond mesh), 8 triangles, one instance, a thin wall collider (`fence = true`: the pedestrians read a wall); granite piers (34 triangles) every 3 .. 5 panels, at every corner above 15 degrees, at the ends of a fence and on both sides of an opening; the 7 stone gates of 1822 (2 piers 0.9 x 0.9 x 3.1 m with a cornice, a capstone and a cast-iron lantern on the five garden gates, plain piers on the private service gate, the iron leaves standing open and folded 2.2 m into the garden) and the green wire-mesh fence of the pitch (w81524430: 3.5 m, 83 closed panels, 88 posts, 5 plain gates). THE BASE is the STREET side: `y = max(0, gardenHeight)`; the plinth reaches 0.3 m under it; along the lake's north shore and the west pond (where the floor lies at -0.2 .. -0.45 against the quay) 68 built panels and their piers stand 0.18 m outward of the outline, and the stub `w112853216` (2.5 m of its 2.7 m lie in the water) is not built. 3 draws (panels, piers, one static mesh with the gates, the leaves and the pitch fence), 15,744 triangles, 1,106 colliders. Fix round 1: everything stone (plinths, piers, gate piers, pitch posts) is ONE opaque static mesh on the outline layer, the pickets (instanced) and the leaves / diamond mesh (static) are the alpha-cut no-outline meshes; the outline pass cannot see cut-outs, so a pier inside the alpha-cut mesh let the outlines of the trees and walkers behind it show on its face. Also in this round: `people/nav.js nav.reliefY(x, z)` (the relief height of the area under a point, null in the strip) lifts the access nodes added after the height pass (benches, bus stops, photo spots) and `people/sim.js` lets a follower of a group stand on the ground under it (not at its leader's height).
* **The furniture** (`furniture.js`): the 132 benches and 167 bins of `data/furniture.js` as `knockInstances` (the strip's pattern: with `ctx.knock` one dynamic `InstancedMesh` per part and a record per piece (the whole garden is ONE knock cell: `FURNITURE_CELL` 4000 m), without it one static mesh per part), vertex-coloured models built with `streets/vb.js` (bench 52 triangles: black cast ends and back posts, orange-brown slats of `props/furniture.js benchPark`; bin 18: the black cast-iron urn, hexagonal; the boxes carry no hidden face). Budget: 14.5 k triangles and 4 mesh draws (+ the night-only light pool) for ALL of furniture.js. A bench faces the nearest way ribbon (by its edge) within 6 m, else the water within 30 m, else the garden centre; an OSM node that stands on a ribbon (a bench's whole footprint at least 0.3 m off the ribbon edge), in the water or in a footprint moves to the nearest clear spot within 3 m (bench) / 4.5 m (bin) (the T5 nudge: 16 benches, largest 1.65 m, 52 bins, largest 3.3 m, none left); every y is `gardenHeight`, and the colliders (absolute y) reach from `min(0, y) - 0.5` to `y` + the height so that a piece on a hill still blocks and is an obstacle of the nav. The benches are published into `ctx.services.landmarks.benches` (`where: 'garden'`, `rotY`, `len`, ground `y`) the way `landmarksE` does: `people/nav.js addBench` gives them seats and an access node (122 of 132 attach), walkers sit on them (`furniture-people-probe`). The 6 other FURNITURE nodes (a picnic table, a bicycle rental, 4 bicycle racks) are not claimed: `props` keeps drawing them.
* **The lanterns**: 31 cast-iron lanterns (a green-black pole of 4.2 m, a four-sided glass lantern and a pyramid roof; 120 + 8 triangles) on the lit non-bridge ways (data/paths.js `lit`: 15 of the 17 lit ways are footways, 8 of them carry one), every 27 m from 8 m on ALTERNATE sides, 0.6 m beyond the drawn ribbon edge (the nominal half width + the +-8 % of paths-draped.js), never on a stair, a bridge deck, within 4 m of a gate, 12 m of another lantern, 6 m of a camera pose, the clearance of a bench / bin / pier, within 6 m of a sharp corner (the draped ribbon cuts it) or on a slope over 0.2; plus 10 smaller lanterns on the five lit gates' piers (not knockable). Night: the glow material follows `ctx.shared.uNight` (the strip's lamp pattern of `streets/lamps.js`), a shared additive light pool (one draw, radius 4.6 m, on the highest ground within its radius) is hidden with a knocked lantern. The lanterns are `lamp` knock records (160 kg, anchored 18 km/h, 3 parts: body, glow, pool).
* **The monuments** (`monuments.js`): the Pioneers-heroes memorial n4186236011 (a tapering limestone stele of 4.8 m on a two-step granite base with a recessed relief panel, a bronze star and flame; a paved apron of 3 rings x 28 draped 7 cm over the plateau M so that the alleys' ribbons (+3 cm, polygon offset) do not cover it; and the limestone CHEEKS of the three descents w425635247 / w1458068086 / w1458068092: two blocks per tread beside the kerbs of the stairs that `paths-draped.js` built, 0.4 m wide, 0.62 m above each tread: the stair geometry is untouched), Chaikovsky n2974761162 (a civil bust of the figure kit on a granite pedestal), Yesenin n4222941889 (a bronze figure of the kit on a plinth), the fountain "Boy with a duck" n950150577 (a granite basin of 2.6 m with a rim 0.5 m high, water 0.12 m under the rim, the bronze boy with his duck on a pedestal, an animated spout and droplets through `ctx.onUpdate`, a paved plaza of 6.2 m), the ORANGERY (the mapped building w23002927, 55.8 x 16.8 m, outside the outline in the north-west corner, CLAIMED by the module so that `city` draws no block there: a brick plinth, an iron frame in 11 bays, a glazed gable roof with a ridge beam, translucent glass, ten palms inside), the pitch (turf in 12 mown bands with the marking lines and two goals at the short ends), the three playgrounds (a rubber / sand surface with a slide, a swing frame, a sandpit, a rocker: 8 structures) and the fitness station (4 bars). 10 draws, 10.9 k triangles (budget 40 k / 12 draws); every monument is published into the shared `ctx.services.landmarks` (monuments and 4 photo spots on the nearest way: the nav gets 83 stands round the monuments and 13 photo views; walkers photograph the memorial, Chaikovsky, Yesenin and the fountain in `furniture-people-probe`).
* **The claims** (`groups.js`, the sub-group `tauride-garden`): the 14 fence ways (the central round's w420650657 / 58 and the pitch ring w81524430 among them), the 11 gate nodes, the 3 monuments and the fountain, the 5 other GARDEN_GREEN records (pitch, 3 playgrounds, fitness station, the lake island's wetland), the 2 hedges w1199664269 / 70 and the orangery building: 483 ids (447 + 36) and one building. The generic modules drew nothing at those ids (`claims-draw-audit --where`: no centroid of `city`, `streets` or `props` on the 4 fence / hedge ids), so the claim is bookkeeping and an exclusion for later; the orangery claim removes a 142-triangle block from `city` and the 5 GREEN claims 72 triangles from `environment`. The SERVICE LANE (w1258039516 / 17) is NOT claimed (decision, `data/furniture-design.js SERVICE_LANE_DECISION`): `streets` keeps drawing its asphalt, the cars stay out through `HOOKS.carKeepOut`.
* **The planting keeps off the furniture**: `vegetation.js` got a POST PASS (no hook, no engine file): a tree whose trunk surface is nearer than `KEEP_OUT.furniture` (0.5 m) to a piece (1,617 discs: 3 per bench, bins, lanterns, piers, closed panels) moves to the nearest clear spot within `furnitureNudge` 2 m or goes; the planting is the T5 planting everywhere else (3 trees moved, none went: `vegetation-test` section 2c). The worn spots of `data/ground.js` for the fountain, the south gate and the memorial are anchored to the OSM node (`follow`, resolved in the entry by `furniture.js resolveWornSpots`), the benches of the ground mask are the planned ones.
* **Knock parity of the garden: closed.** 132 benches + 167 bins + 31 lanterns = 330 records (the North census 4,513 -> 4,843: `north-knock-test` check 2b); the fence panels, the piers and the lanterns on the gate piers are plain colliders (the strip's iron fences are not knockable either). The strip is not touched (`landmarksTauride` is not in its MODULES): its census stays 8,946.
* **No new hook key** (9.1 is unchanged): the fence, the furniture and the monuments are the module's own builders; the only engine-adjacent edits are the post pass of the module's planter and the claims.
* Files: `src/world/landmarksTauride/{fence.js, furniture.js, monuments.js, data/furniture-design.js}` (new), `src/world/landmarksTauride.js`, `{groups.js, vegetation.js, data/vegetation.js, data/ground.js}`, `tools/check.mjs` (BUDGET 765 k -> 815 k: the measured 741,060 + 10 %), `tools/golden/layout-north.json` (claims only); tools `tauride/furniture-test.mjs` (new), `tauride/furniture-people-probe.mjs` (new), `tauride/furniture-preview.{mjs,html}` (new), `north-knock-test.mjs`, `tauride-height-test.mjs`, `tauride/{vegetation-test, ground-test, pond-test, bridge-test}.mjs`. No engine file outside `landmarksTauride/` is edited and `layout.js` gains no export.

### 8.16 T7 of the Tauride remake: integration, the art pass, the engine leftovers

Phase T7 of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (its log, section 12, has the numbers, the decisions, the commands and the open items). Branch `tb-t7`. CPU plus one GPU-lock job (the scratch bake of the North); no GPU frame: the visual QA, the frame times at the 12 poses and the contact sheet are the QA agent's.

* **Wildlife and the garden's trees (closed: TAURIDE-REMAKE 11.11).** `wildlife/world.js`: `landmarksTauride` joins the garden tree-list line (`landmarksE / F / H / J` + it), its trees enter the trunk grid (`inTree`, `treesNear`: ground birds keep off the trunks, sparrows find their shelter) and a crown perch stands at `(t.y || 0) + t.h - 0.6` (the module's trees stand on the relief; the environment's trees have no `y`: unchanged). Strip-neutral: an absent service is an empty list. `tools/wildlife-test.mjs` section 9 (North only) pins the perches, their height over the hills, the grid and `treesNear`, with the list left out as the mutant.
* **Walkers follow the relief (closed: the T2 note "groundY easing").** `people/sim.js` `stepRoute`: after the interpolation of the node heights the branch asks `nav.reliefY(a.x, a.z)` (the ground under the walker; `null` on the strip, where `HEIGHT_PATCHES` is empty, and on a service deck) and uses it as the target height. The interpolation was off the terrain by up to 0.19 m on 6.5 m edges (the memorial plateau; the gate of `people-probe` was 0.5 m, T2 measured 0.43, T6 0.245): in motion now p50 0.025 m, p90 0.056 m, worst 0.17 m. The strip's people-test is like for like (the only differences are timings and the run-to-run scene counts that the clean tree shows too).
* **The landmark door points are pedestrian doors (closed for the doors in a people zone: 10.2 / 10.7 / 11.5).** The fifteenth hook key `HOOKS.doorServices` (9.1; the North: `['landmarksNorthFull', 'landmarksOktyabrsky']`, validated like `deckServices` in `lib/area-tables.js`) names the services whose `entrances` ({ x, z, rotY, bldg }) `people/nav.js` adds to its entrances after the city's (a point needs a finite `rotY`: Smolny, Suvorov and Kirochnaya publish clearing discs without a facing and stay out; the zone test is the city's: `zoneW > 0.3`). Of the 95 points 6 lie in a people zone and 4 became entrances (`nav.stats.landmarkDoors`); 18 distinct walkers headed for them in 3 x 45 s. The strip names no service: no change. `tools/tauride/nav-leftovers-probe.mjs` pins it with two mutants (the hook emptied, the records left out).
* **The view spots.** The Kapodistrias monument (28 stands + 4 photo-spot views) and the garden's monuments and fountain (83 stands, 13 views) already yielded nav spots (the merged shared services); the Church of Simeon and Anna does NOT: its photo spot lies on a 4-node pavement island outside every people zone. It needs a `peopleHot` row in `area.js` (not changed: TAURIDE-REMAKE 11.16).
* **The art pass** (module-local, no engine geometry changed: `treegeo.js` is untouched, the birches use `F.geo`, a row key of the garden's own `SPECIES_EXTRA`): see the log entry for the list with the reference photos; the data are `data/furniture-design.js` (bench colours, `FENCE.low`, `FENCE.extraOpen`, the fountain boy scale, Yesenin, the goals, the orangery coping), `data/bridge-design.js` (the light rails, the pink-brown decks), `data/ponds.js` (`DUCKWEED`), `data/ground.js` (the round plaza), `data/vegetation.js` (the birches, the turned groves, the larch palette), `paths-draped.js` (`PATH_COLORS`), `ground-material.js` (the worn earth), `environment/water.js` (the `tauride-pond` LOOK row, a notch darker: the one engine-file edit of the art pass, a row only the garden reads).
* **The dam's east end is open** (a 14th opening of the fence, `FENCE.extraOpen`), so the walkway of the dam leads to the street; the T6 loosening of `pond-people-probe` is removed.
* **The numbers**: `check:north` landmarksTauride 738,046 triangles (budget 815 k), 154 meshes, 4,016 colliders; the scratch bake of the North 56 files, 30.9 MiB compressed (medium stage 30.1 MiB, + nav-performance 0.79 MiB); `baked-check` 0 failures; `nav-bake-equiv --area north` PASS (25,098 nodes, 39,487 edges); the strip `check` counts identical to the clean tree, `layout-golden` strip and North identical.
* Files: `src/world/{wildlife/world.js, people/sim.js, people/nav.js, lib/area-tables.js, environment/water.js}`, `src/areas/north/hooks.js`, `src/world/landmarksTauride/{fence.js, monuments.js, bridges.js, vegetation.js, ponds.js, paths-draped.js, ground-material.js, data/*}`; tools `wildlife-test.mjs`, `north-hooks-test.mjs`, `north-keepout-test.mjs`, `tauride/{furniture-test, people-probe, pond-people-probe, pond-test, vegetation-test}.mjs`, new `tauride/{nav-leftovers-probe, perf-poses}.mjs`.

### 8.17 T8b of the Tauride remake: the garden nursery (a user fix)

The user's air photo and screenshot of the lot north-west of the garden (between Potemkinskaya, Shpalernaya and the garden's fence): the generic yard ground (mottled grey asphalt, moss and brick) with three 4-storey yellow blocks in it. The module `landmarksTauride/nursery.js` (+ `data/nursery.js`) turns it into the old nursery of the photo. Branch `tb-t8b`, CPU only (the GPU look is the QA agent's). The log entry is in docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md section 12.

* **The ground.** A second garden-owned floor: ONE flat mesh over the lot ring (22 vertices, 35,352 m2, y = 0.005 like the garden floor, polygon offset 2) with its own toon material (the garden's pattern: one category per pixel from the noise tile and a 1 m RGBA8 mask: R gravel tracks, G the dark soil under the glass, B the fenced yard, A dry-grass lanes). The environment ground is cut out under it: `HOOKS.groundHoles` (9.1) hands out TWO rings, the garden outline and the lot ring, each moved in by 6 cm. The lot ring shares the garden outline's west edge (vertices 56 .. 60 of `data/footprints.js GARDEN`) exactly, so the two floors meet without a gap or an overlap; at the streets the sidewalks and footways lie above the floor as above the environment ground.
* **The glasshouses.** Nine (the user's "seven" are the long ones, 3 + 1 + 3, and two cross houses stand across the east end on each side of the concrete box): steel-rib frames (flat strips on three layers 1.8 cm apart, plinth and rails as boxes), a shallow gable, a low concrete plinth, 7,314 panes of which a deterministic hash keeps 1,857 (25.4 %, in patches); one frame mesh (double-sided, no outline), one glass mesh (transparent, opacity 0.42); one box collider per house.
* **The buildings of the lot.** OSM gives `w1457964048`, `w1457964049` (two concrete boxes) and `w30324513` (OSM kind=greenhouse, 121 x 15 m) no height; the generic city drew each as a 4-storey yellow block. The new sub-group `tauride-nursery` of `landmarksTauride/groups.js` claims them (8.3) and the module draws them low and plain (4.4 / 4.8 m flat roofs with a parapet, a 5.4 m annex with a grey gable roof) with their colliders. The known-data route was NOT taken: `src/areas/north/known-data.js` must equal `tools/strip-data-known.mjs --check`, a hand edit fails `area-data-test`.
* **The hooks** (strip-neutral: gated by the module list like the garden's): `treeReject` answers `'nursery'` inside the lot ring (the module plants its own 218 big leafless trees, fix round 1), `tuftReject` and `propReject` cover it (`hooks/keep-out.js`, `inNursery` of the pure data module).
* **Fix round 1 (the QA's two majors).** The yard's classical buildings (`w23002928`, `w23002936`, `w23002941`) and its three small buildings (the grey office `w30324512`, the brown-roofed barn = the garage `w239884014`, a utility cube `w259579860`) are claimed too (`tauride-nursery`: 9 ids) and drawn low: the real OSM rings as two-storey walls with windows, hipped blue-green roofs built from rectangular wings (`data/nursery.js YARD_HOUSES`), one collider box per wing (the courtyard stays open). The trees are big leafless crowns (8 m template, grey-brown twig masses, 6 .. 11 m, 218 trees in the gaps, INSIDE the houses and round the lot, `TREE_ROWS`).
* **Numbers**: the module adds 38,006 triangles in 5 draws after fix round 1 (floor 20, frames 5,150, glass 3,714, trees 27,904, buildings and details 1,218; at the first delivery 20,626 with 11,224 / 518); `check:north` landmarksTauride 738,046 -> 758,672 (budget 815 k -> 835 k), 12 colliders more (4,016 -> 4,028); `layout-golden` North regenerated (GROUPS, SUBGROUPS and the probe set that follows the group anchors: 63 leaves, all of them claims or probes), the strip golden identical.
* Files: `src/world/landmarksTauride/{nursery.js, data/nursery.js, groups.js}`, `src/world/landmarksTauride.js`, `src/areas/north/hooks/{ground.js, keep-out.js}`; tools `tauride/{nursery-test, nursery-preview}.mjs` (new), `north-hooks-test.mjs`, `tauride/{ground-test, pond-test, bridge-test}.mjs`, `check.mjs` (the budget), `golden/layout-north.json`.
### 8.17 T8a of the Tauride remake: three fixes of the owner (the west gate floor, the roof of the garden pavilion, the plate of Yesenin)

Log entry T8a of docs/TAURIDE-REMAKE.md (causes, numbers, commands). Branch `tb-t8a`. CPU only (software GL stand-in frames); the lead rebakes (the city product of the North changes: +70 triangles, the 16th hook key).

* **The z-fight hatching on the alley at the west gate**: `paths-draped.js overlapLevels` gives every way a LEVEL (+0.4 mm, at most 4) so that two ways of ONE class that cover the same ground are never coplanar (the south alley w1051226582 ended on the main alley w81524425 without a shared vertex and ran with it for 5 m in one plane; the same at the south gate, 25 m2, and 3 more gates: 479 m2 of coplanar overlap in the garden, 11 m2 now); a way end that lies on another way fades its tonal bands and rim like a junction end. The pitch turf and the play surfaces (3.2 / 3.5 cm) were 0 .. 3 mm from the ribbons at the four pitch gates and a playground: now 4.6 cm.
* **The transparent roof of the pavilion w420650670**: the deck of the generic city is a one-sided face and the outline pre-pass is double-sided, so a viewer below the eaves saw the sky through the roof and the deck outline in the air; all 13 garden footprints were open from below. The sixteenth hook key `HOOKS.deckUnderside` (9.1) adds a down-facing underside. The strip is unchanged (no key).
* **The plate on Yesenin's pedestal**: `monuments.js plateSpec / plateGeometry / paintPlate`: a dark bronze plate 0.86 x 0.22 m, 8 mm proud of the front face, one 512 x 132 canvas texture painted at load, one draw, 10 triangles (`data/furniture-design.js YESENIN.plate`).
