* Bridges that open: `landmarksTroitsky.js` / `landmarksPalaceBridge.js` skip `stepCar` for `car.floats` (a leaf neither carries nor pushes a boat that floats under it, and the helmsman is not set down as a walker on the leaf).

**Look and sound.** `drive/boat/model.js`: white topsides, a navy hull with a red pinstripe, a pointed bow with a rising sheer, a cream foredeck, a teak cockpit floor, a console with a tilted windshield and a helm (the wheel turns), two bucket seats, a rear bench, a stainless bow rail, a life ring, running lights (red port / green starboard, a white bow and stern light, brighter at night) and an outboard on the transom that swings with the wheel; lofted flat-shaded hull through `ctx.mat.toon` with vertex colours like the cars, 5 draws (body, glass, lamps, helmsman, outboard + wheel) and the wake. `wake.js`: two soft-particle systems of `drive/fx.js` (now exporting `system(ctx, cap, additive, name, flat)`): flat white foam quads behind the quarters (a V) and the transom (a churn), billboard spray at the bow above ~25 km/h; one draw each; the foam of a boat that was left keeps fading. Sound (`drive/sound.js`, procedural, no asset): the profile `BOAT_SOUND` (a low rough four-stroke burble whose cylinder pulses `chug` quicken with the revs, the `wash` of the water along the hull that grows with the speed and replaces the car's wind, now and then a bow slap `splash`); the car voices are untouched.

**R and the hand-off.** R (and the places keys) while on a boat: `drive.place` sets the boat where the spot is water (within 6 m of it), else it LEAVES the boat floating and sets the player on foot at the spot, so R at the start of the game never drops a boat on the pavement. A hand-off made on a boat (`gates.js` sends `car: { id: 'boat', view, d: [0,0,0,0] }`; `boat.damage.state` has the four scalars): at the destination `drive.arrive` brings the boat along when that area has a boat and the arrival is on water, else the player is on foot with the usual toast. The boat does not travel between pages; a destination boat is its own dock's boat.

**Extending (a later water-transport round).** More docks: add rows to `DOCKS` (`{ id, x, z, yaw }`; `id` must be unique and match `/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,32}$/` for the hand-off). A second vessel type: a new entry kind beside `loadBoats` with its own `SPEC`, `tuning` (`createBoatHandling(ctx, tuning)` takes every number of `BOAT_TUNING`), model (the car-model API: `root`, `set(p)`, `setView`, optional `wheels` / `steerWheel` / `driver`) and sound profile; `drive.js` needs nothing beyond `kind: 'boat'` and `car.floats`. Steering the water level: `handling.js` reads only `ph.waterLevel` (waterways, ponds and basins) and `L.inPlay`; a waterway with its own level, a lock or a current is a change there. Known gaps: the tourist boats and their pontoons (`landmarksC/boats.js`, `environment/boats.js`) have no colliders, the boat passes through them (and under the low bridges of the canals it hits their deck colliders, which is right); the ducks and the people on the quay do not react to it; a boat cannot leave the area it was built in.

## 15.2 Admiralty-Haymarket: the third live area (map area 46, page `admiralty.html`, bake `baked/admiralty/`; branch area-admiralty, 2026-10-26)

**Shape.** `node tools/area-shape.mjs --k 46 --js admiralty`: 43 play rectangles A01 .. A43, bbox x -4240 .. -160, z -1440 .. 2080, 7.29 km2 with the water zones (6.47 km2 land alone). The registry row `REGISTRY.admiralty` (id `admiralty`, title Admiralteysky-Sennoy / Admiralty-Haymarket, `mapId` 46, `approxMB` 55 = an estimate, the remap of the first two areas) carries NO ST rectangle and NO edge gate: the lead's integration step adds the gates (the overlaps: Palace-Liteyny 1.44 km2 inside x -2720 .. -160, z -1440 .. 240; Smolninskoye none; the parked strip 2.2 km2; the parked north 0.05 km2). `tools/areas-test.mjs` knows an area without the station (the shared ST rectangle is required of the areas that carry it, an area without ST must not reach the complex); `tools/area-garage-check.mjs` expects ONE car (`lada`) in such an area (13.4). The data was cut with `node tools/area-data-cut.mjs --area admiralty` (12.45 MB in 44 files, 4,763 buildings, 12,075 roads, 2,784 trees; `known-data.js` 507 rows) and the page generated by `npm run areahtml` (`page.json`: 14 true facts, sights admiralty / st-isaac / bronze-horseman / kazan-cathedral).

**Modules** (`src/areas/admiralty/area.js`, 23 in build order; the measured triangles of `node tools/area.mjs admiralty tools/check.mjs --area-modules`): environment 1,084 k, streets 1,103 k, city 1,342 k, landmarksC 105 k (Anichkov Bridge, Beloselsky-Belozersky), landmarksD 173 k (group-aware: the library and the Tolstoy House), landmarksE 317 k, landmarksF 273 k, landmarksG 281 k, landmarksH 462 k (Admiralty, Senate and Synod, Bronze Horseman, Alexander Garden), landmarksI 378 k, landmarksJ 300 k (St Isaac's, Nicholas I, Mariinsky Palace, Astoria), bridges 213 k (with the Blagoveshchensky Bridge), landmarksPalaceBridge 5 k (the opening leaves: the whole deck lies in this territory, as in Palace-Liteyny), props 330 k, wildlife 62 k, drive 15 k, and the five PORTED modules (copied verbatim from the source project, commit 0568f7af, one provenance line each, `OWNS_GROUPS`, whole group lists: every sub-group lies in the data): **landmarksNikolsky** 225 k (St Nicholas Naval Cathedral, bell tower, garden), **landmarksMariinsky** 202 k (theatre, Conservatory, Teatralnaya Square gardens, the skybridge, Mariinsky-II), **landmarksNewHolland** 135 k (the island, the Bottle, the arch, the park), **landmarksYusupov** 80 k, **landmarksCentralSouth** 165 k (Trinity-Izmailovsky Cathedral, the Technological Institute and the two Fontanka bridge embellishments; the Vitebsky station sub-group is not claimed: 200 m outside the data). No `rail`, no landmarksA / B, no metro. Not ported (open): landmarksAcademy (the Sphinx pier lies in the territory, the Academy of Arts 40 m outside it; the module is group-aware by claims, a later round can claim `sphinx-pier` alone), the scripted life of the south-west sights (source people/sw.js), the New Holland slope bank of the Admiralty canal (SLOPE_REACHES `adm-newholland`).

**Engine glue** (shared files, additive and keyed by area tables, so palace / smolny / strip / north build bit for bit as before: `tools/check.mjs` counts compared at 916664d): `WATER.keyByName` (lib/area-tables.js, environment/waterways.js `keyOf`: the RIVER records Kryukov, Admiralty, Novo-Admiralty, East and South canal have a null key in the data) with the waterway rows `kryukov`, `admiralty`, `nh-inner` (levels -2.9 / -2.8 / -2.8), their wall tones (quay.js), rail styles, water looks; `WATER.dataWater` for the Kovsh basin; `environment/claims.js` the Yusupov court ring; `bridges/sites-sw.js` the full 27 sites of the source (the area lists 24 more than the strip), `bridges/sites-south.js` (the six southern Fontanka decks selected from the area's own data, empty elsewhere), `bridges/groups-sw.js` (their claims + the Blagoveshchensky Bridge), `environment/river.js` (zones of those sites, the New Holland footbridge window, the kind `zone` record), and the **Blagoveshchensky Bridge** (`bridges/blag.js`, `long.js`, `sculpt5.js`, `mats-vo.js`, `sites-vo.js` verbatim; its 22 historic standards and tumbas stay solid structures, the wing walls are breakable ghost bays, the plain 8 m rail boxes that stood under its railing are gone: the rail batch's bays hold the colliders).

**World rules in the ported modules.** Water is reachable (the audit of `tools/water-reachable-test.mjs` passes: 16 checks, `npm run water` needs the third area in package.json): New Holland's quay railing along the Kovsh and the two canals (about 700 bays of one 2.4 m texture tile with the posts), the pier railing, the four footbridge parapets and the park fences (pickets / panels) are `knockBays`; Nikolsky's pylon fence and lattice fence, Yusupov's spear fences (a template span per (h, plinth, gap) stretched over the bay, granite posts at the ends) likewise (`lib/fence-bays.js` got `splitBays` and `unitPanel`). Benches, bins and lamps of all four modules are knock records through the new `lib/knock-furniture.js` (the same geometry lists, colliders kept as the record items; New Holland's benches and bins had no collider at all before). Solid on purpose (structures): the walls of the buildings, the courtyard walls and the garden wall of the Yusupov Palace (masonry, away from water), the rusticated colonnade, the pier-head tumbas and corner bastions and the barrier kerbs of the Blagoveshchensky Bridge, the gate posts of New Holland (the module's own hedges and lawns are not colliders). The drawn panels of a broken ghost bay stay drawn (the wing walls of the Blagoveshchensky Bridge), as the bridges' posts and handrails do.

**Checks** (CPU, all green for `admiralty` except the expected missing gates): `layout-test` (23 poses, 0 problems), `views-check`, `landmarks-groups-check` (14 kept modules), `claims-draw-audit` (OK, 3 warnings = lawns an owner draws nothing in, 4 notes: Bolshaya Morskaya 37 is the strip's known finding), `area-garage-check` (one Zhiguli at (-2330, -470)), `water-reachable-test` (the cut check treats a bridge deck as a structure: a low slab bridge over a 10 m diagonal canal), `cross-places-test`, `knock-stands-test`, `import-lint`, `runtime-only-lint`; `areas-test`: 148 of 149, the one failure is the edge gates (`no edge gate palace -> admiralty`, the lead's integration step). `tools/area-data-test.mjs --area admiralty` fails the three checks that are written for the North (the Tauride bbox, Chernyshevskaya, the unsplit arrays) and the 600 m water-ring check for the Fontanka piece at (-800, -1520), like smolny.

**Trams.** `ZONES.tramLines`: route 3 and route 16 (4 routes, 12.5 km of the data's tram tracks run through the territory; `buildTramNet` finds both with their terminals at the east and south-west edges of the data).

**Cars and start.** One purple Zhiguli at the spawn (-2330, -470) in the Alexander Garden; HERO = the garden looking at the Admiralty; the view keys are 1 .. 0 / Shift + 1 .. 9 for the sights of the district plus three Places entries (not shown in the HUD list while the place teleports are off, 13.5).

**Risks / open.** The city module builds 1.34 M triangles (the largest of the three areas), the environment 1.09 M: memory and the bake size are not measured (no bake was made); `approxMB` 55 is an estimate. Palace Square, the Hermitage and the Alexander Column lie in the 120 m margin or the Palace-Liteyny territory; the Admiralty and Palace-Liteyny both build G, I, F, E, C and the Palace Bridge (the same claims). The map `maps/intro-draft-src/build-intro.mjs` lists the live areas P and S only (it needs the git-ignored `raw/` data to rebuild).

## 15.x Vladimirsky-Ligovka: the fourth live area (branch `area-vladimirsky`, map area 97)

**Shape.** `REGISTRY.vladimirsky` (`vladimirsky.html`, `baked/vladimirsky/`, `requireBake`, `mapId` 97): the 48 rectangles V01..V48 of `node tools/area-shape.mjs --k 97 --js vladimirsky` (land outline plus the water zones, x -1440 .. 3040, z -480 .. 2640) and the pinned station rectangle `ST` (the outline already contains it; the row keeps it for the rule of 13.9). 8.63 km2 (map: 7.35 km2 of land and overlaps). No edge gates yet: the integration step adds them. Territories that overlap it (10 m grid): Palace-Liteyny 1.22 km2 and Smolninskoye 1.59 km2, 0.40 km2 of each is the station rectangle; no other live area. Map neighbours that are not live yet: 45, 22, 90, 96 (Smolninskoye, live), 98 (Palace-Liteyny, live).
**Data.** `tools/area-data-cut.mjs --area vladimirsky` (source commit 0568f7af): 4,379 buildings (3,482 with the centroid in the play rectangles), 12,349 roads, 10.4 MB raw / 1.67 MB gzip (palace 11.0 / 1.74, smolny 8.4 / 1.32), `known-data.js` 43 rows. `tools/area-data-test.mjs --area vladimirsky`: 18 of 18 (the two North-only sight checks are per area now).
**Modules** (`src/areas/vladimirsky/area.js`, the order of the page; triangles from `node tools/area.mjs vladimirsky tools/check.mjs --area-modules`): environment 661,724; landmarksEast 146,442 (Lavra gate church, Trinity Cathedral, Lavra wings, Feodorovsky Cathedral, Hotel Moscow: all five sub-groups); eastBridges 71,732 (44 bridge ways east of x 1000: the Alexander Nevsky Bridge, the Obvodny canal / Lavra crossings); landmarksNorthFull 120,096 (`north-podpisnye` only); streets 1,133,437; city 1,432,842; rail 565,212 (every rail way of the data, the Vitebsk / Baltic lines south of the station included); landmarksA 295,044 (station, square, Galereya, Oktyabrskaya); landmarksB 171,602 (all four sub-groups); landmarksC 102,062; landmarksD 300,686 (all four sub-groups); landmarksE 91,802 (theatre, Anichkov, Ostrovsky houses); bridges 46,122 (12 sites); props 240,032; traffic 5,798; wildlife 61,052; drive 45,706; people builds nothing on the CPU. No metro network, no cars elsewhere than the four at the station (GARAGE is the palace one).
**Enabled / ported.** (1) `landmarksD`: `groups.js` exports the other three sub-groups (`VLADIMIRSKY_GROUP`, `TOLSTOY_GROUP`, `DOSTOEVSKY_GROUP`, `GROUPS_LIST`; `LIBRARY_GROUP` unchanged); the module was group-aware already (parts gated by the claimed sub-groups); the church-yard fence (`fences.js`, region `church yard`, group `vladimirsky`) is now a row of knock bays (`lib/fence-bays.js`, 12 km/h), the library / hospital fences of Palace-Liteyny stay solid (their output is unchanged). (2) `landmarksE` is group-gated like landmarksD (`PARTS` carry their sub-group; Palace-Liteyny claims all six, so nothing changes there). (3) `bridges/sites-south.js` (ported from the source, picks the real OSM decks by the facade): the Lomonosov, Leshtukov, Semenovsky and Gorstkin bridges on the Fontanka and the Borovoy, Novo-Kamenny, Ippodromny, Predtechensky, Karetny, Ruzovsky, Mozhaysky, Gazovy bridges on the Obvodny canal (12 flat decks with walk ramps); `bridges/sites.js` takes a south site only where the area lists its id in `GROUPS.bridges` (`BRIDGE_GROUP_KEYS`); `environment/river.js bridgeZones()` opens the quay under them. (4) The Obvodny canal is drawn as a `neva` trench (as in the source): `WATER.riverKeys` / `WATER.riverBridgeZones` in `lib/area-tables.js`, used by `environment/waterways.js keyOf` and `river.js riverBridgeZones()` (the eastBridges decks over the canal make auto quay openings); the other areas leave both tables out and are unchanged (`check.mjs --area-modules` of palace and smolny: identical triangle, mesh and collider counts). Water rule: `water-reachable-test` 15 of 15 (1,141 water cells, the Fontanka and the Obvodny canal / Neva bodies each 100 % connected).
**Checks run** (CPU): `check --area-modules`, `landmarks-groups-check`, `layout-test`, `views-check` (14 poses), `area-garage-check`, `water-reachable-test --quick`, `claims-draw-audit` (3 waivers for lamps / a mast that stand in the wall mass of the Atrium and the Lavra wings, `GENERIC_WAIVERS`), `cross-places-test`, `areas-test` (148 of 149: only the missing edge gates fail), `import-lint`, `runtime-only-lint`, `owns-table --check`.
**Known gaps / for the owner.** The railway bridges over the Obvodny canal (American bridge, Tsarskoselsky) have rails only, no deck or girders; the Ruzovsky / Mozhaysky / Gazovy bridges stand in the 120 m margin at the south-west edge; the Catherine Garden, Gostiny Dvor and the National Library (r419489) belong to Palace-Liteyny; `rossi-street` of the source's landmarksE is not in the strip's module (generic buildings); the Fontanka bridges and the Obvodny decks are plain flat `rcarch` / `italyansky` decks, no heritage detail (the source adds `landmarksCentralSouth/bridge-details`); (`package.json` `water` lists palace and smolny only: since 15.y it runs all four live areas.)

**Merge with Admiralty-Haymarket (union).** The two areas had ported the southern bridges and canals independently; the merged tree serves both. `bridges/sites-south.js` computes a site for every named OSM deck in the area's data extent (the Vladimirsky edition: the Anichkov Bridge and the ways of eastBridges are left to their owners) and `bridges/sites.js` adds only the ones whose id the area lists in GROUPS.bridges (Admiralty: the six Fontanka decks of groups-sw.js GROUPS_SOUTH; Vladimirsky: its SOUTH_GROUPS). `WATER.keyByName` (Admiralty) and `WATER.riverKeys` / `WATER.riverBridgeZones` (Vladimirsky) are separate tables, both read by `waterways.js keyOf`; the barrier booms stay as on the fix-bridges branch (the lighter-boom variant of this branch was dropped).

## 15.y The four-area gate graph

**The graph.** Four areas are live (palace, smolny, vladimirsky, admiralty); the parked strip and north keep no gate. Every pair whose territories (`REGISTRY[id].play`, the union of rectangles) overlap by an area gets an edge gate each way, a pair that does not overlap gets none: `tools/areas-test.mjs` computes the overlap from the registry rectangles and checks the rule both ways (exactly one edge gate per overlapping ordered pair, none for a pair that does not overlap, none from or to a parked area). The overlaps in km2 (rectangle intersection; the station rectangle ST is counted where two territories share it): palace-smolny 2.09, palace-vladimirsky 1.87, palace-admiralty 1.44, smolny-vladimirsky 2.32, admiralty-vladimirsky 0.98, **admiralty-smolny 0.00** (no common ground, no gate). That makes five pairs and **ten** gates (the vladimirsky and admiralty runs had measured the two thinnest pairs without ST; admiralty-vladimirsky does overlap, along the Fontanka south of the Anichkov Bridge, x -1440 .. -160, z -480 .. 1120).

| gate | from -> to | reverse | hops (m of boundary) | dead (m) | worst dead strip | landing within 100 m of the reverse trigger |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| e1 | palace -> smolny | e2 | 4040 | 440 | 220 m (P03.z0) | 240 m (5.9 %) |
| e2 | smolny -> palace | e1 | 4000 | 880 | 260 m (S12.z0) | 240 m (6.0 %) |
| e3 | palace -> admiralty | e4 | 4520 | 700 | 120 m (P32.z1) | 0 m |
| e4 | admiralty -> palace | e3 | 5020 | 880 | 160 m (A02.z0) | 0 m |
| e5 | palace -> vladimirsky | e6 | 4120 | 1080 | 160 m (P27.z1) | 80 m (1.9 %) |
| e6 | vladimirsky -> palace | e5 | 2780 | 1040 | 220 m (V08.z0) | 100 m (3.6 %) |
| e7 | smolny -> vladimirsky | e8 | 5960 | 880 | 240 m (S35.z0) | 80 m (1.3 %) |
| e8 | vladimirsky -> smolny | e7 | 3960 | 520 | 220 m (V04.z0) | 80 m (2.0 %) |
| e9 | admiralty -> vladimirsky | e10 | 2400 | 1460 | 260 m (A16.z0) | 40 m (1.7 %) |
| e10 | vladimirsky -> admiralty | e9 | 2420 | 1500 | 240 m (V25.x0) | 0 m |

(`hops` = boundary of FROM where the edge rule holds in some direction, `dead` = ground of TO behind a wall that does not hop, `worst dead strip` = the longest run of it; the rest of the boundary, 11.3 .. 15.4 km per gate, is the world edge with no ground of TO behind it. Numbers of `edgeLens(FROM, TO)`, 20 m steps, printed by `node tools/areas-test.mjs`.)

**The lens thresholds are per gate and unrelaxed.** Rule of the test (unchanged): at least 1000 m of hopping boundary, no dead strip over 300 m, at most 10 percent of the hopping boundary within 100 m of the landing where the reverse gate would fire again. `tools/areas-test.mjs` holds them in `LENS_GLOBAL` and reads a per-gate table `LENS_RELAX` (gate id -> partial thresholds, measured numbers in a comment) for a pair whose overlap is legitimately thin; all ten gates pass the global rule (tightest: e9 2400 m of hops, 260 m worst dead strip at e2 / e9, 6.0 % bounce share at e2), so the table is **empty**. The arrival check (the same x, z, yaw resolved with the DESTINATION layout at every working point, walk / fly / car, dry and wet) runs for every gate in a child per destination area and is 100 % valid.

**Station rectangle.** `ST` (x 400 .. 835, z 160 .. 1060, the Moscow station complex whole) is carried by exactly the areas whose territory reaches it: palace, smolny, vladimirsky, the identical rectangle in all three; admiralty has none and stays clear of it (test: "every live area whose territory contains it carries the SAME ST rectangle").

**Three-way overlap.** At the station complex three territories overlap; the gate machine takes the FIRST row of the FROM area that triggers, in registry order (e1 before e5 for a walker of palace who heads for the shared ground). A hop that lands in a second overlap does not chain: of the 16 two-hop combinations only three (e4 then e5, e6 then e3, e10 then e4) have landings (20 m each, out of 2420 .. 5020 m) from which the next gate would fire at once. `tools/gates-test.mjs` runs the machine with all the real edge rows of an area against ~40 working points of every gate: a gate of the player's own area always offers (all 388 of 388 points), the gate asked for fires at 20 .. 44 of 29 .. 50 of them (the rest are handed to the neighbour row that comes first).

**Tools.** `node tools/edge-smoke.mjs --from <id> --to <id> [--ret]` (real browser, GPU, baked pages) crosses any pair that has a gate (default palace -> smolny; it scans the territory of FROM for a start 80 m before a point that is 60 m inside TO, heading east, west, north or south). `npm run water` runs the water test in all four areas; `garage`, `crossplaces`, `layout`, `landmarks`, `claims-draw` use `each-area.mjs` and need no list.

**Admiralty bridge floors (water-reachable-test check 9, 59 ways).** Two drivable OSM bridges dropped a car into the water. (1) **Lomonosov Bridge** (`w375288599`, 8 cells): the bridge IS drawn (the southern site `south-r5586238` of `bridges/sites-south.js`, listed in GROUPS_SOUTH), but its deck stopped 12 m short of the west end of the way. Cause: the data cut of Admiralty-Haymarket splits the Fontanka polygon (`r1201709`) into four pieces whose rims overlap and touch at region seams (one at x = -880, under the bridge); `bridges/arch.js waterAt` (the bank interval a deck is built between) and its copy in `streets/decks.js` sorted the crossings of ALL pieces together and took consecutive pairs, so the interval around the bridge axis ended at the seam (-18.5 .. 30.2 m instead of -25.9 .. 30.2 m as in Vladimirsky, whose polygon is whole). Fix: the crossings are paired per polygon (even-odd, outer ring and holes together) and the intervals of the pieces united (overlap or a gap under 0.5 m); the result is identical for a single polygon. Compared before and after over every site of every area (strip, north, palace, smolny, vladimirsky, admiralty: 1,893 samples along the cross axis) exactly one site in one area changes: Lomonosov in Admiralty, to the interval of Vladimirsky. (2) **Smezhny Bridge** (`w384987418`, 11 cells, the Fontanka at the Krasnoarmeysky Bridge, x -2807, z 1586): not in the site list (its water is not a named waterway of `sites-south.js`), nothing drew a deck: a floor-only `BRIDGE_ZONES` row `smezhny` (`floor: true`, c [-2807.4, 1585.7], axis 64.8, w 14.5, len 24) in `src/areas/admiralty/area.js`, the same mechanism as the Palace rows of 15.x. Check 9 is green: admiralty 17 checks passed (the palace 22, smolny 18, vladimirsky 16 stay green). The bake of Admiralty and, because `arch.js` / `decks.js` are shared, the one global revision are stale until the next bake.


## 15.z The draft intro map shows the four live areas (branch `map-live-areas`)
`maps/intro-draft.html` (sources `maps/intro-draft-src/`) marked only P and S as live. `build-intro.mjs` now derives the live list from `src/areas/registry.js` (rows with a `mapId`, not `parked`; the badge letters in its `LIVE_LETTER` table): P Palace-Liteyny (98), S Smolninskoye (96), A Admiralty-Haymarket (46), V Vladimirsky-Ligovka (97), with the walkable territory (union km2 of the `play` rectangles: P 7.29, S 9.60, A 7.29, V 8.63), approxMB, sights and the edge-gate neighbours (15.y). The page draws the dashed territory, an orange outline of the district, a `Live` badge in the list and a counter, a `Live scene` block on the district card, and a legend / layer label for P S A V. P and S were rebuilt from the current registry (their old rectangles were from an earlier cut). Only the `exist` percentages of 13 districts, `areas[].live` (4) and `existing` differ in the embedded data; the rest of the map is byte-identical. `maps/n4spb-area-map.html` is untouched. No game files changed, so no version bump.

## 15.z Academy sphinxes: the Egyptian sphinx pier and the Academy of Arts in Admiralty-Haymarket (branch `feat-sphinxes`, the owner's "also add the Academy sphinxes")

**What.** Module `landmarksAcademy` (source commit 0568f7af, `landmarksAcademy.js` + `landmarksAcademy/*`, 29 files, `OWNS_GROUPS = true`) is ported into Admiralty-Haymarket for TWO of its five sub-groups: `sphinx-pier` (the two Amenhotep III sphinxes on red-granite pedestals, the stair pit cut into the Neva wall with its flights, landing, two bench recesses with four bronze griffins, two bronze lamp columns, the railing) and `academy-of-arts` (the Academy, Universitetskaya Embankment 17: the whole OSM relation r165182 with its three parts is in the data, `city-a11.js`). The module builds a part only when the AREA claims its sub-group (`layout.GROUPS.landmarksAcademy`, 2 rows of `landmarksAcademy/groups.js GROUPS_LIST`); `academic-garden` (w82802146 and w82802189 are absent from the data), `trezzini-square` and `dom-akademikov` (100+ m outside the play rectangles) are not claimed: no partial building anywhere. Palace-Liteyny, Smolninskoye and Vladimirsky-Ligovka do not list the module: their module triangle counts are identical before and after (`check.mjs --area-modules`, every module of the three pages). Measured in admiralty: landmarksAcademy 117,210 triangles (academy 62,748, pier 54,590 incl. 2 x 11.2 k for the sphinxes), 147 colliders, 8 knock records (BUDGET 135 k); environment -702 (the pit), streets -17, city -2,764 (the generic blockout of the Academy is claimed away), props -530.

**Where.** The pier lies inside the territory: the pit ring (-3397.2, -501.2) .. (-3358.5, -514.8) is 40 .. 46 m from the nearest play rectangle edge (A11 / A12), the sphinxes (-3397, -497) and (-3360.5, -519) 44 .. 46 m, the pedestals about 50 m. The territory is unchanged. The Academy lies entirely OUTSIDE the play rectangles, in the 120 m scenery margin (the centroid (-3431, -597) is 0 .. 22 m beyond A10 / A11 / A12; the front stands 22 .. 100 m from the pavement of the embankment); it is seen from the pier, never reachable. Its north tip (z -687) is 7 m beyond the data extent: a building is cut whole by its centroid, so `tools/landmarks-groups-check.mjs` got `TEST.extentSlack { group: metres }` (the Academy: 30 m) instead of a failing rule.

**Engine glue** (additive, keyed by two new area tables, inert in every other area): `WATER.ownerRings` (the stair pit, `landmarksAcademy/groups.js WATER_RINGS`) and `WATER.keepOut` (`KEEP_OUT`: no generated descent / landing stage / boat at the pier front) in `lib/area-tables.js`. `environment/waterways.js` turns an owner ring into Neva water (a hole in the ground, the water surface, `layout.inRiver`, no bank edge of its own) and exports `ownerWater / ownerWaterAt / keepOut`; `environment/quay.js` opens the generic quay at the ring (gapAt: no coping, parapet or collider within 0.5 m; no wall face inside the ring + 6 cm; the pieces are cut at both edges: `frames(bank, splits, tests)` of the source project) and skips the generated descents / sites in the keep-out; `boats.js` keeps the keep-out; `streets/bld.js` stops the pavement at the pit like at a quay; `environment/claims.js` keeps trees / hedges / tufts off the paved strip in front of the Academy (`PAVED_RINGS`, only while `landmarksAcademy` is in the build list). The three pieces of the source project's `owners.js` that were not needed (BANK_RINGS, the streets `waterclip.js`, the island water) are not ported.

**World rules in the module** (changes against the source, all in `landmarksAcademy/pier.js`): the two stretches of black cast-iron railing along the pit are rows of breakable bays (`lib/fence-bays.js knockBays`, 3 bays of 2.8 m each, collider + knock record per bay: a car breaks a bay off at the foot, the gap is passable); the two bronze lamp columns are knock records (`lib/knock-furniture.js`, kind `lamp`; their granite blocks stay a solid 1.1 m structure); the invisible guard box along the last tread ("nobody walks on the water") is GONE: a walker steps off the lower flight into the Neva (test 2 of `tools/academy-pier-test.mjs`); the two street-level shelves behind the railing were invisible 2 m walls (the east one also 2.2 m off its slab), they are walkable pavement now (floor at y 0.03), so the railing bays are the only barrier and knocking one opens the way. Solid on purpose (granite structures, none thin or tall): the sphinx pedestals and the sphinxes (monuments), the granite benches of the recesses with the griffin plinths (built into the back wall of the stair), the cheek walls of the lower flight, the lamp blocks. The Academy's walls are building colliders. The water-reachable audit (admiralty 17 checks) passes without an ALLOW row: nothing of the pier is a thin tall collider.

**HUD, views, labels.** HUD rows before the river rows: "Academy of Arts" (the claimed building), "Egyptian Sphinxes" (circle r 30) and the "Universitetskaya Embankment" road row (before this, the Fontanka row named the Neva quay of the pier "Fontanka": its `shoreDist < 14` test covers every quay of the area, the other Neva stretches still read "Fontanka" there: a known quirk, not touched); a Places-only view `Place.sphinxes` (-3407.5, -497.9) looking at the stair pit with the west sphinx six metres to the right; the loading label row `landmarksAcademy`. `TEST.claimed` has the Academy row, `mustHaveAreas` two rows.

**Checks (CPU).** `check.mjs --area-modules` (admiralty: 24 modules, landmarksAcademy 117,210 within the budget), `landmarks-groups-check` (OK), `layout-test` (OK, 24 poses), `views-check` (24 poses, 0 failing), `water-reachable-test --quick` (17 passed), `claims-draw-audit` (OK, 4 warnings: the 3 lawns of before and the steps way w628771368, which the pier draws below the street, outside the audit's height band), `owns-table --check` (OK), `import-lint`, `runtime-only-lint`, `knock-stands-test`; the new `tools/academy-pier-test.mjs` (4 checks: pit = Neva water, walk down the flight into the river, railing stops / knocked opens, lamps and pedestals). `areas-test` and `cross-places-test` fail the same rows as before this branch (generated pages on disk, the cross-place rows: the lead's step). The Admiralty bake and the one global revision are stale until the next bake (new files, `quay.js`, `waterways.js`).

## 15.z Hand-off car (branch `fix-handoff-car`)
Owner: "when I cross the border of the new areas in a car and a loading happens, my car disappears." **Root cause.** `drive.arrive()` does `enter(ho.car.id)`; `enter` finds the car only in the page's `fleet`, which is built from the area's `GARAGE.poses`. Admiralty keeps ONE purple Zhiguli (`lada`; the four cars stand only at the station), so a coupe / pickup / roadster driven across e3 / e10 into it was not in the fleet: `enter` returned false, `arrive` returned false and `main.js` put the player on foot with the toast (the car was gone). Measured by `tools/handoff-car-test.mjs` on the old tree: 69 of 92 arrivals into admiralty without the car (every non-Zhiguli). The four-car destinations (palace, smolny, vladimirsky) put the player into the car in every simulated crossing (street approaches at 8 / 22 / 40 m/s through the real gate machine, the page's `planArrival`, the real drive module): not reproducible on the CPU there; the one cause left that only a real browser shows is not covered.
**Fix.** `loadGarage(ctx, { carry })` (`drive/cars/index.js`): `drive.js` passes the car of a car hand-off (`ctx.handoff.ho.car.id` with the arrival pose); when the area's garage has no pose for that car it is built anyway, parked at the arrival pose, `def.carried = true`, and gets no parked box until it is left somewhere (`exit` adds it). An area that keeps the car is unchanged, a page without a hand-off has exactly its own fleet; the four-cars-at-the-station rule is about parked cars, a DRIVEN car may exist elsewhere. Not carried: the speed (the car arrives at rest, as before). A boat id is not a car and is not carried (a boat hand-off onto a page without that boat still puts the player on foot).
**Test.** `npm run handoffcar` (`tools/handoff-car-test.mjs`, 15 / 19 checks per area, ~30 s each, CPU).

## 15.z Boat look fixes (branch `fix-boat-look`)
Four owner requests on the motor boat, all in `drive/boat/*`, `drive/sound.js`, `drive.js` (no bake). **Sound:** `BOAT_SOUND` is about 9 dB down (`volume` 0.9 -> 0.32), lower (fundamental 26..106 Hz, was 32..152), softer (triangle + quiet saw + sine sub, low-pass 120..~700 Hz, Q 0.8, deeper `chug` 0.45, `intake` 0.2); the wash is quieter and darker (`wash` 0.17, new profile field `washLp` [300, 700]), the bow slaps lower / softer (`splashHz`, `splashVol`); the car profiles do not have these fields and are untouched. **No driver when unused:** the helmsman mesh is hidden at build and shown only while driven: `model.setDriven(bool)` (called by `drive.js` `enter` / `exit`, so respawn R, hand-off and a refused hand-off all end hidden) plus `driven` in the per-frame pose; the first-person view still hides it. **Transparent sides:** the cockpit liner stopped at station 4, so the hull side between z 0.10 and 0.95 (beside the seats and the console) had NO inner wall and the single-sided outer side was culled from inside; the liner also poked out of the hull at the stern (teak corners beside the transom), the transom top and the front-wall / foredeck ridge had slits, and the bow strips were wound inside out. Fixed in `hullGeometries()` (liner follows the hull, runs over every cockpit station, stern cap, ridge-following front wall, hints per panel) and the hull body is `side: 'double'` as a second line of defence. `tools/boat-test.mjs` section 10 audits it (edge winding, open edges are T-junctions, liner inside the hull, 3600 rays from inside / outside: 0 back-face-first hits; the old hull gave 482). **Windshield:** a flat unlit `MeshBasicMaterial` (tint `#7f9bb0`, opacity 0.22, no sky reflection / fresnel / streaks) instead of the shared `mat.glass` shader with its world-anchored highlight streaks; the frame is unchanged.

## 15.z Prachechny Bridge drawn (branch `fix-prachechny-look`, the owner's report of 2026-10-26, pose -831.1, 1.7, -1689.7, yaw 352.2: "bridge looks strange")

The floor-only rows of 15.x gave the Prachechny Bridge a car floor but no look: a flat road slab over the water with no thickness, no parapet, no arch, an unfinished quay edge. Now the three bridges that had floor-only rows in Palace-Liteyny are **sites of the bridges module**, drawn with the kit of the Mars Field reach: `bridges/sites-pal.js` (`PAL_SITES`, frames and deck zones taken from the OSM deck relations r3808074 / r3808095 / r15750441 and the road and footway ways), `bridges/groups-pal.js` (`GROUPS_PAL`: the claims, appended by `src/areas/palace/area.js` to its `GROUPS.bridges`; `sites.js` builds exactly the PAL sites an area lists, so no other area changes: admiralty / vladimirsky / smolny `check.mjs --area-modules` give identical triangle counts) and `bridges/styles-pal.js`.
* **Prachechny (Laundry) Bridge** `prachechny`: style `arch3` (three unequal pink-granite arches 9.6 / 12.6 / 9.6 m scaled to the 37 m bank interval of the Fontanka mouth, pier 2.2 m with round / pointed cutwaters, oval lucarnes in the spandrels, arch soffit, voussoir rings, granite fascia with a rolled cornice) with a solid rolled parapet in breakable sections along both faces (the way the other arch3 bridges do it) and, new, four capped granite corner posts (0.95 m, 1.4 m) at the heads (`site.posts`, `styles2.js arch3`). Zones: asphalt exactly the street's 12 m (the road meets the deck without a step), pavements 2.4 / 2.0 m.
* **Upper Swan Bridge** `swan-upper`: the existing `swan-upper` style (grey-pink humpback arch, solid 0.8 m granite parapet with square end pylons), fixed bank interval (the data polygon of the Swan Canal ends inside the deck).
* **2nd Engineer Bridge** `eng2`: a shallow granite arch over the moat (1.2 m below the street), black `barsE` bar railing between granite corner posts, pink granite wing parapets.
Everything else is the generic kit: every parapet section and railing bay is a knock record, the face colliders are ghost bays, the deck is chunked walk ramps over the banks (the water under the arches stays open and reachable; the quay opening of each deck is a `BRIDGE_ZONES` row with `own: true`; `river.js SITE_OF` maps the zone ids). The three floor-only rows are gone (the deck is its own car and walker floor); the `floor: true` mechanism stays for the Admiralty `smezhny` row.
Checks: `node tools/area.mjs palace tools/water-reachable-test.mjs` 22 / 22 (check 9: 33 bridge ways keep the road level, the Prachechny both ways at 6 and 12 m/s), `check.mjs --area-modules` palace OK (bridges module +8.9 k triangles for the three bridges), landmarks-groups-check, layout-test, claims-draw-audit OK; a CPU walk over each deck (road and both pavements, both directions) never leaves the deck level. The bake of Palace-Liteyny is stale after this change (not re-baked here).
